In this paper, we present ISU-Test, an automated testing approach that combines rendering-based scene generation with search-based testing to evaluate ISU systems. By framing testing as an optimization problem and systematically modifying scene parameters, our method generates diverse in-car scenarios and explores a wide range of configurations. We evaluate ISU-Test on both an industrial prototype and open-source VLMs across two case studies: question answering and captioning, comparing against randomized scenario generation. Results show that ISU-Test significantly outperforms the baseline, achieving up to 10 times higher failure rates and up to 3.6 times higher failure coverage.

</details>


### [106] [InvSplat: Inverse Feed-Forward Scene Splatting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02301)
*Polina Karpikova,Wenjing Bian,Haofei Xu,Hendrik Lensch,Andreas Geiger*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出InvSplat，一种前馈式多视图逆渲染框架，直接预测带有材质属性的结构化3D高斯表示（含反照率、金属度、粗糙度等），实现单次前向传播中联合预测几何与反射参数，支持重光照和新视角合成，在合成和真实数据集上展现出优于2D基线方法的多视图一致性、准确的材质恢复和稳定的新视角渲染。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有逆渲染方法中，基于优化的方法需要逐场景拟合，成本高昂；基于图像空间的学习方法缺乏显式3D表示，多视图不一致且新视角渲染不稳定。亟需一种前馈式、支持多视图一致且具有显式3D表示的逆渲染方法。

Method: 提出前馈式多视图重建框架InvSplat，直接预测带有材质属性的结构化3D高斯表示；每个高斯基元参数化为均值、法线、不透明度、旋转、尺度、反照率、金属度和粗糙度；集成材质估计网络的先验与多视图3D重建骨干网络，实现单次前向传播联合预测几何与反射参数。

Result: 在合成和真实数据集上，相比2D基线方法实现了更好的多视图一致性、准确的材质恢复、稳定的新视角渲染，支持基于物理的重光照，对视角依赖效应的建模比现有基于RGB的前馈重建方法更忠实。

Conclusion: InvSplat通过结构化的3D高斯表示与材质属性的联合预测，有效解决了逆渲染中多视图不一致和缺乏显式3D表示的问题，为前馈式逆渲染提供了新范式。

Abstract: Inverse rendering aims to recover both 3D geometry and physically meaningful material properties from images, enabling applications such as relighting and novel view synthesis. Optimization-based methods achieve high fidelity but require costly per-scene fitting, while image-space learning-based approaches often suffer from multi-view inconsistencies and lack an explicit 3D representation for stable novel view rendering. We present a feed-forward multi-view reconstruction framework for inverse rendering that directly predicts a structured 3D Gaussian representation with intrinsic material attributes. Each Gaussian primitive is parameterized by mean, normal, opacity, rotation, scale, albedo, metallic, and roughness, enabling a disentangled and physically grounded scene representation. Our model integrates priors from a material estimation network with a multi-view 3D reconstruction backbone, allowing joint prediction of geometry and reflectance parameters in a single forward pass. Experiments on synthetic and real-world datasets demonstrate improved multi-view consistency compared to 2D baselines, accurate material recovery, and stable novel view rendering. Our representation further supports physically-based relighting and more faithful modeling of view-dependent effects compared to existing RGB-based feed-forward reconstruction methods. Our project webpage is: $\href{https://poliik.github.io/invsplat/}{\text{https://poliik.github.io/invsplat/}}$.

</details>


### [107] [NEvo: Neural-Guided Evolutionary Video Synthesis for Dynamic Visual Selectivity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02317)
*Yingtian Tang,Sogand Salehi,Ming Zhou,Amir Zamir,Leyla Isik,Martin Schrimpf*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出一种新颖的神经引导视频合成框架，通过进化搜索结构化提示空间，结合动态编码模型生成针对特定视觉脑区的最优动态刺激，在腹侧、背侧和外侧通路中恢复已知选择性，并揭示沿外侧通路对社会-动态特征敏感性逐步增强的新发现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大脑通过层级组织的功能特化区域处理动态视觉输入，但先前的脑编码模型研究主要局限于静态图像，动态视觉处理尚未充分探索。

Method: 提出神经引导视频合成框架：在结构化提示空间中进行进化搜索，由预测体素级响应的动态编码模型引导，最大化目标ROI的预测活动以发现超激活动态刺激。

Result: 合成视频持续超越人工制作的定位视频；恢复腹侧、背侧和外侧通路已知选择性；揭示对时间动态敏感性的系统性差异；搜索光分析发现外侧通路对社会-动态特征的渐进性复杂化处理。

Conclusion: 该框架实现了对动态视觉选择性的计算机内探索，为未来的活体实验提供了新的预测方向。

Abstract: The human brain processes dynamic visual input through hierarchically organized, functionally specialized regions. While recent in silico brain encoding models can synthesize optimal stimuli to probe selectivity in different brain regions, prior work has been largely limited to static images, leaving dynamic visual processing underexplored. We introduce a novel neural-guided video synthesis framework that generates stimuli optimized for target brain regions across visual cortex. Our method performs evolutionary search over a structured prompt space, guided by a dynamic encoding model that predicts voxel-level responses to video inputs. By maximizing predicted activity for a target ROI, the framework efficiently discovers hyper-activating dynamic stimuli that consistently surpass handcrafted localizer videos. The synthesized videos recover known selectivities across ventral, dorsal, and lateral pathways, and further reveal systematic differences in sensitivity to temporal dynamics. A searchlight analysis provides new insight into the progression toward increasingly complex social-dynamic features along the lateral stream, further supported by probing with synthesized abstract, non-naturalistic stimuli. Taken together, our framework enables in silico exploration of dynamic visual selectivity, with new predictions for in vivo experiments

</details>


### [108] [GAP-GDRNet: Geometry-Aware Monocular Visual Pose Sensing on a Single-Target Synthetic Spacecraft Dataset](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02360)
*Yonglong Zhang,Yang Liu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出GAP-GDRNet，一种基于几何感知注意力机制的帧，用于非合作航天器交会中的单目RGB 6D位姿感知。该方法在GDR-Net基础上增加了注意力特征细化（AFR）模块和分块几何自注意力（PGSA）模块，以增强弱纹理、薄附件等挑战场景下的位姿估计性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在非合作交会和在轨服务中，单目相对位姿感知是关键问题。但航天器图像存在弱表面纹理、薄附件、光照变化和部分遮挡等挑战，导致几何特征稀疏且不稳定，现有方法难以鲁棒地估计6D位姿。

Method: 基于GDR-Net的几何引导直接回归范式，在密集几何预测前插入注意力特征细化（AFR）模块，在Patch-PnP中插入分块几何自注意力（PGSA）模块。AFR增强全局航天器结构和局部弱纹理线索，PGSA关联下采样后的几何分块以辅助最终位姿回归。使用基于Blender的标注流程生成训练所需的掩码、密集模型坐标图、相机内参和6D位姿标签。

Result: 论文未在摘要中明确给出具体数值结果，但提出该方法能有效应对弱纹理、薄附件、光照变化和部分遮挡等挑战。

Conclusion: GAP-GDRNet通过引入注意力机制增强了GDR-Net在非合作航天器场景下的6D位姿感知能力，AFR和PGSA两个模块分别从全局-局部特征增强和几何分块关联角度提升了位姿估计的鲁棒性。

Abstract: Monocular relative pose sensing is a central perception problem in non-cooperative rendezvous and on-orbit servicing. In spacecraft images, however, weak surface texture, thin appendages, illumination changes, and partial occlusion often leave only sparse and unstable geometric evidence. This article presents GAP-GDRNet, a geometry-aware attention-enhanced framework for monocular RGB-based 6D pose sensing. The method follows the geometry-guided direct regression paradigm of GDR-Net and modifies two points in the pipeline: an attention-based feature refinement (AFR) module is placed before dense geometric prediction, and a patch-level geometric self-attention (PGSA) module is inserted into Patch-PnP. AFR reinforces global spacecraft structure together with local weak-texture cues; PGSA then relates downsampled geometric patches before final pose regression. A Blender-based annotation process supplies target masks, visible-region masks, dense model-coordinate maps, camera intrinsics, and 6D pose labels for supervised training.

</details>


### [109] [VisionAId: An Offline-First Multimodal Android Assistant for People with Visual Impairment, Featuring Personalized Object Retrieval](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02371)
*Cristian-Gabriel Florea,Stelian Spînu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: VisionAId是一款将普通智能手机转变为实时视觉助手的Android应用，集成了6个端侧深度学习模型（单目深度估计、实例分割、视觉/面部嵌入、人脸检测、定制钞票检测），通过ONNX Runtime运行，并支持可选的云端大语言模型进行场景描述和自动标注。其核心创新是少样本流水线，用户拍摄物体多角度照片后，系统可通过AR标记、空间音频和距离振动引导用户找到该物体，所有反馈均为多模态（罗马尼亚语语音、语音指令、振动）。在Samsung Galaxy S21 Ultra上，INT8量化将深度延迟从~1200ms降至~491ms，钞票检测mAP@50达0.986，3米内深度误差<1cm。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 全球超过2.85亿视障人士在日常任务（避障、定位物品、识别人脸、处理现金）中面临障碍，现有辅助应用受限于预定义类别、依赖云端或需专用硬件，缺乏实时、全本地化且支持个性化物品识别的综合解决方案。

Method: 开发Android应用VisionAId，集成6个端侧深度学习模型（单目深度估计、实例分割、视觉/面部嵌入、人脸检测、定制钞票检测）全部通过ONNX Runtime本地运行；可选云端LLM（Gemini Flash）用于场景叙述和自动标注；提出少样本流水线：用户从多角度拍摄目标物体，系统学习后可在环境中定位该实例，并通过AR标记、空间音频和距离比例振动引导用户；所有反馈采用多模态（罗马尼亚语音合成、语音指令、振动）。

Result: 在Samsung Galaxy S21 Ultra上：INT8量化将深度模型延迟从~1200ms降至~491ms；定制钞票检测器mAP@50达0.986；3米内度量深度校准误差<1cm。

Conclusion: VisionAId通过将6个深度学习模型完全本地化运行并集成少样本个性化物品定位流水线，证明了普通智能手机无需专用硬件即可实现实时、多模态的视觉辅助，显著提升视障人士的自主性。

Abstract: Over 285 million people worldwide live with a visual impairment, for whom everyday tasks such as avoiding obstacles, locating personal belongings, recognizing familiar faces, or handling cash remain persistent obstacles to personal autonomy. Existing assistive applications are typically limited to recognizing predefined categories, depend heavily on cloud connectivity, or require dedicated hardware. We present VisionAId, an Android application that turns a commodity smartphone into a real-time visual assistant. The system integrates six on-device deep learning models (metric monocular depth estimation, instance segmentation, visual and facial embeddings, face detection, and a custom banknote detector) running entirely through ONNX Runtime, with an optional cloud large language model (Google Gemini Flash) used only for narrative scene description and automatic object labeling. A distinctive contribution is a few-shot pipeline for personal objects: the user photographs an object from several angles, and the system later locates that specific instance in the environment, guiding the user toward it with augmented-reality markers, spatial audio, and distance-proportional haptics. All feedback is multimodal (Romanian speech synthesis, voice commands, vibration). On a reference device (Samsung Galaxy S21 Ultra), INT8 quantization reduces depth latency from ~1200 ms to ~491 ms, the custom banknote detector reaches an mAP@50 of 0.986, and metric depth is calibrated to below 1 cm of error within 3 m.

</details>


### [110] [Learning Spectral and Polarimetric Clues for One-to-Multimodal Novel View Synthesis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02372)
*Federico Lincetto,Gianluca Agresti,Mattia Rossi,Piergiorgio Sartor,Pietro Zanuttigh*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出SPoILeR方法，通过学习多模态间的互相关，在只有RGB图像监督下（或极少量非常规模态数据），即可对红外、偏振、多光谱等非常规模态进行多视图一致渲染，无需昂贵传感器和标定设置。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有神经渲染技术虽已扩展到多光谱、红外、偏振等成像模态，但均依赖昂贵的传感器和标定设置来捕获新场景的多模态数据，成本高昂且难以推广。

Method: 提出SPoILeR方法，利用多模态预训练阶段学习不同模态间的互相关，然后在微调阶段仅以RGB图像为监督，预测非常规模态的渲染结果。

Result: 实验表明，该方法能在完全没有对应传感器输入样本的场景中，准确渲染红外、偏振和多光谱帧。

Conclusion: SPoILeR通过模态间相关性学习，实现了仅用RGB图像即可预测非常规模态渲染，大幅降低了对专用传感器和数据采集的需求。

Abstract: Neural rendering techniques allow for accurate reconstruction of the geometry and color appearance of 3D scenes. Some methods have extended their use to additional imaging modalities, such as multispectral, infrared, or polarimetric data. However, all of these approaches require expensive sensors and calibrated setups to capture new multimodal frames for each new scene. We propose Spectral and Polarimetric Implicit Learned Representation (SPoILeR), a novel method to obtain multi-view consistent renderings of unconventional modalities for scenes where either only RGB frames or very few of the additional modalities are available. Thanks to a multimodal pre-training phase, the model learns the mutual correlation between different modalities. This step allows predicting accurate renderings of unconventional modalities during a fine-tuning phase supervised only by RGB images. Experimental results show that the approach can accurately render infrared, polarimetric, and multispectral frames for scenes where no input sample captured by these types of sensors is provided.

</details>


### [111] [Representation Distribution Matching for One-Step Visual Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02375)
*Lan Feng,Wuyang Li,Eloi Zablocki,Matthieu Cord,Alexandre Alahi*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文系统研究了表示分布匹配（RDM）范式，发现经典MMD在正确估计后成为强大可扩展的目标，生成批次存在最优值（>2048），单一表示易被欺骗需多编码器平衡。提出的改进RDM（iRDM）在ImageNet上达到单步生成SOTA（SW_r14 1.30），并能将FLUX.2四步生成器后训练为单步生成器（GenEval 0.826 vs 0.794），仅需90 H200 GPU小时。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索表示分布匹配（RDM）范式的设计空间，系统研究其关键设计轴（分布比较方式和表示空间），并解决单一表示易被欺骗的问题，以实现更强的单步图像生成。

Method: 沿两个设计轴（分布比较方式、表示空间）进行受控实验。采用正确估计的MMD作为目标，使用大规模批次（>2048），并构建平衡的多编码器电池进行匹配，同时使用SW_r14（14个编码器上的Sliced-Wasserstein距离）作为独立评估指标。

Result: iRDM在ImageNet上SW_r14达1.30，超越此前单步SOTA；PickScore偏好测试中71.2%样本优于此前最佳单步生成器。将FLUX.2四步生成器后训练为单步生成器，GenEval 0.826（超四步版0.794），PickScore 22.76（超四步版22.58），仅需90 H200 GPU小时。

Conclusion: 经典MMD在恰当估计下可成为可扩展的强目标；生成批次需远超常规大小；单一表示不可靠需多编码器平衡。iRDM证明了RDM范式在高效单步生成中的巨大潜力。

Abstract: We elucidate the design space of Representation Distribution Matching (RDM), our name for the paradigm that trains a one-step image generator by matching generated and reference feature distributions under frozen pretrained encoders. We identify two design axes, how the distributions are compared and the representations they are compared in, and controlled studies along them yield three findings. First, the classical MMD, which could not train convincing generators a decade ago, becomes a strong and scalable objective once estimated right. Second, the generated batch is then the operative variable, with an optimum above 2048, far beyond customary batch sizes. Third, any single representation can be gamed, driven below the real score while images stay visibly fake, so we match against a balanced battery of encoders and evaluate with SW_r14, a Sliced-Wasserstein distance over 14 encoders that is independent of the training loss and resists gaming. Combining the preferred choices yields improved RDM (iRDM): it sets the one-step state of the art on ImageNet at SW_r14 1.30, corroborated by PickScore, a human-preference proxy our objective never optimizes, which prefers it over the prior best one-step generator on 71.2% of matched samples. The same recipe post-trains the four-step FLUX.2 [klein] into a one-step generator, surpassing the four-step version on GenEval, 0.826 to 0.794, and on PickScore, 22.76 to 22.58, in 90 H200 GPU-hours. Project page: https://alan-lanfeng.github.io/rdm/.

</details>


### [112] [OrbitQuant: Data-Agnostic Quantization for Image and Video Diffusion Transformers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02461)
*Donghyun Lee,Jitesh Chavan,Duy Nguyen,Sam Huang,Liming Jiang,Priyadarshini Panda,Timo Mertens,Saurabh Shukla*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: OrbitQuant提出了一种数据无关的权重量化方法，通过归一化旋转基（RPBH旋转）使激活分布稳定，无需重新拟合校准数据即可跨时间步、提示词和模态通用，在多个DiT模型上实现了SOTA的PTQ性能，并首次将图像DiT量化推进到W2A4可用水平。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 扩散Transformer（DiT）虽在图像/视频生成中表现优异，但多步采样和高参数量导致推理成本高昂；后训练量化（PTQ）虽是天然解决方案，但DiT激活值随时间步、提示词和引导分支变化，现有方法需为每个新检查点或模态重新拟合校准数据，缺乏通用性。

Method: OrbitQuant采用数据无关的权重量化方法：在归一化旋转基（随机置换分块Hadamard旋转，RPBH）下量化，该旋转使各坐标围绕固定的已知边际分布，从而单个Lloyd-Max码本即可覆盖所有时间步、提示词和层；对权重行离线将旋转吸收到权重中，推理时仅需对激活做前向旋转。

Result: 在FLUX.1、Z-Image-Turbo、Wan 2.1和CogVideoX模型上，多个低位宽设置下达到PTQ的SOTA效果；首次将图像扩散Transformer的PTQ推进到W2A4（权重2比特、激活4比特）并保持可用生成质量；无需逐模态调参即可从图像任务迁移到视频任务。

Conclusion: OrbitQuant通过数据无关的旋转基量化策略，消除了DiT PTQ中对校准数据的依赖，实现了跨时间步、跨模态、跨模型的通用量化方法。

Abstract: Diffusion transformers (DiTs) achieve state-of-the-art image and video generation, but their multi-step sampling and growing parameter count make inference expensive. Post-training quantization (PTQ) is the natural remedy, yet DiT activations shift across timesteps, prompts, and guidance branches, forcing prior methods to re-fit calibration data for every new checkpoint or modality. We present OrbitQuant, a data-agnostic weight-activation quantizer that bypasses range estimation by quantizing in a normalized, rotated basis. In this basis, a randomized permuted block-Hadamard (RPBH) rotation concentrates each coordinate around one fixed, known marginal regardless of the input, so a single Lloyd-Max codebook serves all timesteps, prompts, and layers of a given input dimension. We extend the same quantizer to weight rows offline, absorbing the rotation into the weights so that it cancels inside each linear layer and only a forward rotation on the activations remains at runtime. The same recipe transfers from image to video with no per-modality tuning. Across FLUX.1, Z-Image-Turbo, Wan 2.1, and CogVideoX, it sets the state of the art for PTQ at several low-bit settings. It also pushes PTQ of image diffusion transformers to W2A4 with usable generation quality.

</details>


### [113] [Transformer Geometry Observatory TGO-II: Representational Similarity Observatory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02386)
*Kaustubh Kapil,Kishor P. Upla*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出TGO-II框架，通过CKA、SVCCA、TwoNN-ID和token协方差分析，系统研究了ViT在监督训练中表示几何的演化。发现：层间表示逐渐特化、内在维度持续增加后稳定、token强交互结构贯穿训练全程。这表明表示复杂度与层特化同时出现，流形扩展不依赖于token解耦。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究主要关注注意力机制和下游性能，对Transformer内部表示几何在训练过程中的演化缺乏深入理解。

Method: 提出TGO-II框架，采用CKA、SVCCA、TwoNN-ID和token协方差分析四种度量方法，对ViT-Small/16在监督训练中的表示几何进行系统分析。

Result: 三个关键发现：1) CKA和SVCCA随训练持续下降，表明层间表示逐渐特化；2) 内在维度先增加后稳定，表示流形逐步扩展；3) token协方差和耦合分析表明强token交互结构持续存在。

Conclusion: 表示复杂度与层特化同时出现，流形扩展不以token解耦为前提，提出新假设：ViT通过逐步丰富的变换增加表示复杂度，同时保持强token交互结构。

Abstract: While Vision Transformers have achieved remarkable success across computer vision and language applications, the geometric evolution of their internal representations throughout training remains insufficiently understood. Existing analyses primarily focus on attention mechanisms and downstream performance, leaving the evolution of representation geometry largely unexplored. In this work, we present Transformer Geometry Observatory-II (TGO-II), a representation geometry analysis framework designed to investigate how Transformer representations evolve during supervised training. TGO-II analyzes Vision Transformer (ViT-Small/16) representations using Centered Kernel Alignment (CKA), Singular Vector Canonical Correlation Analysis (SVCCA), Two-Nearest Neighbor Intrinsic Dimensionality (TwoNN-ID), and token covariance analysis. Our experiments reveal three key observations. First, both CKA and SVCCA progressively decrease throughout training, indicating increasing representational specialization across Transformer layers. Second, intrinsic dimensionality consistently increases before stabilizing, suggesting progressive expansion of the representation manifold into a larger set of locally accessible degrees of freedom. Third, token covariance and coupling analyses demonstrate that strong token interaction structure persists throughout training, challenging the hypothesis that increasing representational complexity arises primarily from progressive token independence. These findings suggest that representation complexity and layer specialization emerge simultaneously during training. Manifold expansion appears to occur without token decoupling. Together, these observations motivate a new hypothesis in which Vision Transformers increase representational complexity through progressively richer transformations while preserving strong token interaction structure during learning.

</details>


### [114] [Show Me Examples: Inferring Visual Concepts from Image Sets](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02402)
*Nick Stracke,Kolja Bauer,Stefan Andreas Baumann,Miguel Angel Bautista,Josh Susskind,Björn Ommer*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出VICIS任务评估VLMs从图像集合中推断视觉概念的能力，并设计训练框架和架构来学习从图像集中提取概念特定嵌入并应用于新查询，实验表明该方法能生成更准确多样的输出并泛化到未见概念和模态。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视觉-语言模型(VLMs)虽然能遵循复杂文本指令，但难以仅从视觉上下文中进行推理，特别无法从一组示例图像中推断共享概念并将其应用于新输入。这限制了模型在纯视觉场景下进行概念归纳和迁移的能力。

Method: 1) 定义VICIS任务：给定少量共享概念的图像作为上下文集和一个查询图像，模型需生成保留上下文概念且与查询一致的新图像；2) 设计训练框架和架构，从图像集合中学习推断视觉概念，并从查询中提取概念特定嵌入。

Result: 在合成数据和ImageNet/WordNet大规模数据上，所提模型能生成更准确和多样化的输出，并泛化到未见概念和模态（如草图）。而现有最先进VLMs在此任务上表现不佳，常忽略视觉上下文或产生有偏见的生成。

Conclusion: 该工作揭示了当前VLMs在纯视觉概念推理上的不足，提出的VICIS任务和相应方法为提升模型从图像集合中学习并迁移视觉概念的能力提供了有效途径。

Abstract: Vision-language models (VLMs) can follow complex textual instructions, yet they struggle to reason from purely visual context. In particular, current models fail to infer shared concepts from sets of example images and apply them to new inputs. We introduce Visual Concept Inference from Sets (VICIS), a task that evaluates this capability. Given a small context set of images sharing a concept and a query image, the model must generate new images that preserve the context-defined concept while remaining consistent with the query. We show that state-of-the-art VLMs perform poorly on this task, often ignoring the visual context or defaulting to biased generations. To address this gap, we propose a training framework and architecture that learn to infer visual concepts from image sets and extract concept-specific embeddings from queries. Experiments on synthetic data and large-scale ImageNet/WordNet data show that our model generates more accurate and diverse outputs and generalizes to unseen concepts and modalities such as sketches.

</details>


### [115] [Combating Textual Noise and Redundancy: Entropy-Aware Dense Visual Token Pruning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02484)
*Xuehui Wang,Xuankun Yang,Wei Shen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出了一种基于熵感知的密集剪枝框架EADP，通过统计熵过滤文本噪声和子模最大化实现结构化视觉token选择，在严格预算下提升VLM精度-效率权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视觉token剪枝方法在处理密集指令和细粒度查询时，因文本噪声污染跨模态评分和标准token选择导致特征碎片化，无法保留关键视觉线索。

Method: EADP框架包含两个核心步骤：1）利用统计熵量化和过滤文本噪声，生成鲁棒的细粒度指令相关性分数；2）将token选择建模为带空间先验的子模最大化问题，确保整体且非冗余的视觉表示。

Result: 在严格token预算下鲁棒保留细粒度视觉线索，在多个具有挑战性的多模态基准上取得SoTA性能，显著改善VLM的精度-效率权衡。

Conclusion: EADP通过将剪枝重构为结构化压缩问题，有效解决了文本噪声和特征碎片化两个瓶颈，为视觉token高效剪枝提供了新范式。

Abstract: Visual token pruning is a crucial strategy for accelerating VLMs by compressing redundant image patches, yet existing methods often fail to preserve critical cues under dense instructions and fine-grained queries. In this paper, we investigate this failure and identify two underlying bottlenecks: the widespread dispersion of textual noise that corrupts dense cross-modal scoring, and the feature fragmentation inherent to standard token selection. To address these issues, we propose Entropy-Aware Dense Pruning (EADP), a framework that reformulates pruning as a structured compression problem. EADP first leverages statistical entropy to quantify and filter out textual noise, yielding a robust, fine-grained instruction relevance score. Subsequently, instead of naive Top-K selection, EADP casts token selection as a submodular maximization problem with a spatial prior, explicitly ensuring a holistic and non-redundant visual representation. Extensive experiments demonstrate that EADP improves the accuracy-efficiency trade-off of VLMs, robustly preserving fine-grained visual cues under strict token budgets while achieving SoTA performance on challenging multimodal benchmarks.

</details>


### [116] [Object-centric LeJEPA](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02404)
*Jakob Geusen,Ender Konukoglu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于LeJEPA的目标级自监督学习方法，通过使用SAM生成的廉价目标掩码，将图像级对比学习扩展到目标级表示对齐，避免了场景分割与表示学习的循环依赖问题，在多个下游任务上超越图像级方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 图像级自监督方法（如LeJEPA）在下游任务中表现良好，但通常需要大量训练数据。目标级对齐理论上更数据高效，但完全自监督地进行场景分割和目标表示学习存在循环依赖的不稳定性。本文旨在规避这一不稳定性，同时利用目标级对齐带来的数据效率优势。

Method: 在训练期间使用现成的SAM生成的目标掩码作为给定输入，将LeJEPA的分布抗坍塌目标从整图自然扩展到可变大小的目标集合。额外引入实例分离损失，将同一场景中的其他目标作为负样本，进一步提升下游性能。

Result: 在两种模型规模和10-100%的COCO数据上，目标级LeJEPA在DAVIS（跟踪）、ImageNet-1k（分类）、ADE20k（分割）和NAVI（重识别）上均优于图像级LeJEPA。

Conclusion: 通过利用现成的目标掩码（如SAM），可以稳定地将图像级自监督方法扩展到目标级表示学习，在多个下游任务上取得更优性能，验证了目标级对齐在数据效率上的优势。

Abstract: Image encoders trained with LeJEPA can deliver strong features for downstream tasks, but, like other image-level self-supervised methods, typically require large training datasets. Aligning representations at the level of objects rather than whole scenes promises greater data efficiency, but doing this in a completely self-supervised way, effectively jointly partitioning a scene and representing its objects, is unstable: the two are locked in a cyclic dependency, partitioning requires meaningful representations, while meaningful representations require consistent partitioning. We sidestep this instability by taking object masks as given during training, using cheap, off-the-shelf SAM proposals. We extend LeJEPA - whose distributional anti-collapse objective ports naturally from whole images to variable-sized sets of objects - to align object-centric representations rather than whole images. An additional instance-separating loss, which treats other objects in the same scene as negatives, further boosts downstream performance. Across two model scales and 10-100% of COCO, object-level LeJEPA outperforms image-level LeJEPA on tracking (DAVIS), classification (ImageNet-1k), segmentation (ADE20k), and re-identification (NAVI).

</details>


### [117] [Wavelet-Guided Semantic Signal Compensation for Inversion-Free Image Editing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02421)
*Anqi Tang,Wenhao Sun,Zhaoqiang Liu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出了一种免反演、频率感知的语义补偿策略，通过在生成早期增强有效信号来提升全局编辑能力，同时保持背景保真度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有免反演编辑框架（如FlowEdit）在全局属性偏移时，早期时间步的编辑轨迹难以有效偏离源分布，导致全局修改受限。

Method: 分析发现高噪声区域中主流的流形趋向流会削弱文本条件方向的影响，据此提出频率感知的语义补偿策略，在生成早期阶段增强文本条件信号，同时保持背景结构一致性。

Result: 该方法在不牺牲背景保真度的前提下，显著提升了全局编辑能力。

Conclusion: 通过频率感知的语义补偿有效解决了免反演编辑中全局修改不足的问题，实现了更优的编辑效果。

Abstract: Text-guided image editing aims to modify visual content according to a target prompt while preserving the background. Recent inversion-free image editing frameworks such as FlowEdit have demonstrated strong editing capability without requiring inversion. Empirically, FlowEdit can achieve substantial semantic changes under appropriate hyperparameter settings. However, we observe that under certain global attribute shifts, the editing trajectory may not effectively move away from the source distribution in the early timesteps. Our analysis suggests that in the high-noise regime, the dominant manifold-seeking flow toward the data manifold can reduce the influence of the text-conditioned direction, leading to limited global modification while background structures remain only moderately preserved. Inspired by this observation, we propose an inversion-free, frequency-aware semantic compensation strategy that strengthens the effective signal in the early stage of generation, while maintaining structural consistency in the background. The proposed method improves global editing capacity without sacrificing background fidelity.

</details>


### [118] [Learning to Evolve Scenes: Reasoning about Human Activities with Scene Graphs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02425)
*Francesca Pistilli,Simone Alberto Peirone,Giuseppe Averta*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出SG-Ego（基于Ego4D的时空场景图标注集）和GLEN（图模型处理场景图序列），并定义活动驱动的图编辑预测任务A-GEF，实现可解释的结构化场景动态推理，在多项基准上取得优异表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法依赖隐式视觉或语言对齐表示，缺乏对场景动态的结构化推理；而显式、组合式、可编辑的人-环境交互表示对于丰富的活动理解至关重要。

Method: 1) 构建SG-Ego大规模标注集，将Ego4D扩展为时空场景图，关系三元组随时间融合为场景状态的显式演化描述；2) 提出GLEN图模型，处理场景图序列，实现文本动作对齐与时序演化建模；3) 定义A-GEF新任务，将场景动态建模为由动作驱动的结构化变换序列。

Result: GLEN在EgoMCQ、EgoCVR、EXPLORE-Bench及新提出的A-GEF基准上均取得强于原始视频基线的结果，尤其在推理任务中媲美通常仅由MLLMs处理的性能，同时支持可控且结构化的场景动态预测。

Conclusion: 时空场景图及其推理模型可作为视频理解中强大的组合式、可解释表示，其应用潜力可能超越视频领域。

Abstract: Understanding human behavior while interacting with the surrounding world is crucial for many applications of embodied AI. First-person videos are particularly informative for this problem, as they well capture how activities reshape the scene over time. However, existing approaches often rely on implicit visual or language-aligned representations, disregarding structured reasoning over the scene dynamic. We argue that explicit, compositional and editable representations of human-environment interactions can play a crucial role for rich grounded activity understanding. To this end, we introduce SG-Ego, a large scale annotation set extending Ego4D with spatio-temporal scene graphs, where relations triplets are consolidated over time into explicit time-evolving descriptions of the scene state. To reason over this representation, we propose GLEN, a graph-based model that operates over scene graph sequences to both align them with textual actions and model their temporal evolution. In addition, we formulate the activity-driven graph-edit forecasting (A-GEF) problem, a novel task that casts scene dynamics as a sequence of structured transformations conditioned on ongoing actions, enabling explicit reasoning about how scenes change over time. We validate our approach across multiple downstream tasks, spanning retrieval benchmarks as EgoMCQ and EgoCVR, as well as long-horizon reasoning benchmarks as EXPLORE-Bench and the newly introduced A-GEF. GLEN achieves strong results compared to raw video baselines and it excels in reasoning settings, typically addressed only with MLLMs, while enabling controllable and structured predictions of scene dynamics driven by human activities. We believe our results establish spatio-temporal scene graphs, together with models that reason over them, as strong compositional and interpretable representations for video understanding and potentially beyond.

</details>


### [119] [Interpretation-Oriented Cloud Removal via Observation-Anchored Residual Flow with Geo-Contextual Alignment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02471)
*Ziyao Wang,Maonan Wang,Yucheng He,Xianping Ma,Ziyi Wang,Hongyang Zhang,Yirong Cheng,Man-on Pun*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出GACR框架，通过OAR-Flow将云去除重构为物理引导的残差反演过程，并结合GCPA利用视觉基础模型保持语义完整性，在六个云去除数据集和十二个下游任务上验证了其优越的重建质量与下游任务精度提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有云去除方法过分追求视觉真实感，忽视了后续分析任务（如语义分割、变化检测）对语义结构完整性的需求，导致语义漂移和下游性能退化。

Method: 提出Geo-Anchored Cloud Removal (GACR)统一框架，包含两个核心组件：(1) Observation-Anchored Residual Flow (OAR-Flow)，将生成轨迹锚定在有云观测而非纯噪声上，实现物理引导的残差反演；(2) Geo-Contextual Prior Alignment (GCPA)，利用视觉基础模型在语义流形上约束重建过程，保持空间-语义完整性。

Result: 在六个云去除数据集和十二个下游任务上的大量实验表明，GACR在产生更优重建质量的同时，持续提升了下游任务精度。

Conclusion: GACR通过联合优化重建保真度与可解释性，有效弥合了云去除与下游遥感解译之间的语义鸿沟，代码已开源。

Abstract: Cloud removal (CR) is essential for optical remote sensing, serving as a prerequisite for reliable downstream interpretation, such as semantic segmentation and change detection. However, existing CR approaches often prioritize visual realism while overlooking their impact on subsequent analytical tasks, leading to semantic drift and degraded downstream performance. To address this issue, we propose Geo-Anchored Cloud Removal (GACR), a unified framework that jointly ensures faithful reconstruction and robust interpretability. At its core, GACR incorporates Observation-Anchored Residual Flow (OAR-Flow), which reformulates CR as a physically grounded residual inversion process. By anchoring the generative trajectory to the cloudy observation rather than pure noise, OAR-Flow enables fast, stable, and faithful reconstruction. To further preserve semantic structures critical for downstream interpretation, GACR integrates Geo-Contextual Prior Alignment (GCPA) to constrain the reconstruction within a semantic manifold induced by a Vision Foundation Model (VFM). Consequently, GACR strictly maintains the spatial-semantic integrity of complex landscapes. Extensive experiments across six CR datasets and twelve downstream tasks demonstrate that GACR produces superior reconstruction quality while consistently improving downstream task accuracy. The code is available at https://github.com/wzy6055/GACR.

</details>


### [120] [EAGLE-360: Embodied Active Global-to-Local Exploration in 360$^\circ$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02479)
*Jingtao Xu,Zizhuo Lin,Jianwen Sun,Yi Yang,Yawei Luo*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出EAGLE-360，一种面向360°全景主动视觉搜索的全局到局部探索框架。通过适应RoPE Rolling位置编码建模全景连续性，结合大规模数据集（14K+全景图、70K+轮VQA对话）以及SFT+GRPO训练管线，在360°视觉搜索任务上实现了近8倍的准确率提升，并显著提高了探索效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有MLLM在360°全景环境中进行主动视觉搜索时存在根本性局限：无法有效建模全景特有的极地畸变和连续柱面拓扑结构，导致目标检测精度显著下降。现有方法依赖碎片化局部视角、刚性初始化且缺乏全局全景先验，导致探索短视低效，且目标出视野时难以鲁棒恢复。

Method: 提出EAGLE-360框架，核心包括：(1) 全局到局部探索范式，利用全局先验建立初始整体视角，迭代推理并逐步缩小搜索空间；(2) RoPE Rolling坐标偏移位置编码机制，建模全景连续拓扑；(3) 构建包含14,000+张4K全景图和70,000+轮高质量VQA对话的大规模数据集；(4) 采用SFT（监督微调）与GRPO（组相对策略优化）结合的训练管线，激发复杂空间推理和工具调用能力。

Result: 在360°视觉搜索任务上，EAGLE-360相比基础模型实现了近8倍的准确率提升，同时显著增强了探索效率，建立了新的最先进水平（SOTA）。

Conclusion: EAGLE-360通过全局到局部的主动探索范式、面向全景的位置编码设计以及大规模数据与强化学习的训练策略，有效克服了标准MLLM在360°全景环境中的根本性局限，为主动视觉搜索任务提供了高效的解决方案。

Abstract: While Multimodal Large Language Models (MLLMs) have demonstrated exceptional capabilities in standard visual understanding, adapting them for active visual search in 360$^\circ$ panoramic environments exposes fundamental limitations. Specifically, standard MLLMs struggle to effectively model inherent panoramic properties, such as severe polar distortion and continuous cylindrical topologies, which significantly degrades target detection accuracy. Consequently, existing panoramic search methods attempt to compensate by relying heavily on fragmented local viewpoints. Burdened by rigid initialization and a lack of global panoramic priors, these approaches suffer from myopic, inefficient exploration and struggle with robust error recovery when targets are out of view. To overcome these challenges, we propose EAGLE-360, a novel Embodied Active Global-to-Local Exploration framework. Rather than performing exhaustive local searches, EAGLE-360 leverages global priors to establish an initial holistic perspective, iteratively reasoning and progressively narrowing the search space. Architecturally, we adapt RoPE Rolling, a coordinate-shifting positional encoding mechanism, to seamlessly model the continuous topologies of panoramas. To facilitate this paradigm, we construct the large-scale EAGLE-360 dataset, comprising 14,000+ 4K panoramas and 70,000+ rounds of high-quality VQA dialogues. By employing a training pipeline that integrates Supervised Fine-Tuning (SFT) with Group Relative Policy Optimization (GRPO), we effectively elicit complex spatial reasoning and tool-calling capabilities. Extensive experiments demonstrate that EAGLE-360 establishes a new state-of-the-art for 360$^\circ$ visual search, achieving nearly an 8-fold increase in accuracy over the base model while significantly enhancing exploration efficiency.

</details>


### [121] [GeoMix: Descriptor-Free Visual Localization via Global Context and Multi-Detector Training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02486)
*Yejun Zhang,Xinjue Wang,Zihan Wang,Esa Rahtu,Juho Kannala*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: GeoMix提出了一种无描述符的视觉定位框架，通过局部几何增强、全局上下文建模和多检测器混合训练，显著缩小了与基于描述符方法的精度差距，在多个数据集上将旋转误差降低89%、平移误差降低90%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 无描述符视觉定位在效率、隐私和地图维护方面具有优势，但其精度远落后于基于描述符的方法。根本原因在于仅依赖几何信息的匹配缺乏足够的几何可判别性：现有方法未充分利用局部几何线索、缺乏关键点间的全局上下文，且过度依赖单一关键点检测器。

Method: GeoMix从三个层次增强几何可判别性：(1) 局部层面：设计方向感知和距离感知嵌入，丰富邻域聚合的细粒度空间结构；(2) 全局层面：引入可学习的上下文节点，通过交叉注意力聚合和分发场景范围信息，解决局部感受野外的模糊性；(3) 训练层面：提出Mix-Training，利用无描述符方法天然的检测器无关几何空间，在多个关键点检测器上联合学习表示。

Result: 在MegaDepth、Cambridge Landmarks、7Scenes和Aachen Day-Night数据集上，GeoMix达到无描述符方法的最优性能，75百分位旋转误差降低89%，平移误差降低90%，并零样本泛化到未见过的检测器，显著缩小了与基于描述符方法的差距。

Conclusion: 通过在局部、全局和训练三个层面系统增强几何可判别性，GeoMix有效弥合了无描述符与基于描述符视觉定位方法之间的精度鸿沟，证明了纯几何匹配在视觉定位中的巨大潜力。

Abstract: Descriptor-free visual localization eliminates high-dimensional descriptor storage, preserves scene privacy, and simplifies map maintenance, yet its accuracy still lags far behind descriptor-based pipelines. We identify this gap to insufficient geometric discriminability in geometry-only matching. Without visual appearance, current methods underutilize local geometry cues, lack the global context among keypoints, and overfit to a single keypoint detector. We further observe that descriptor-free matching naturally enables multi-detector training, as heterogeneous keypoints can be optimized in a shared geometry-only space without aligning descriptor spaces. Building on these insights, we propose GeoMix, a descriptor-free 2D-3D matching framework that strengthens geometric discriminability at three levels. Locally, directional and distance-aware embeddings enrich neighborhood aggregation with fine-grained spatial structure. Globally, learnable context nodes aggregate and redistribute scene-wide information via cross-attention to resolve ambiguities beyond local receptive fields. At the training level, Mix-Training exploits this detector-agnostic geometry space to learn representations across multiple keypoint detectors. Extensive experiments on MegaDepth, Cambridge Landmarks, 7Scenes, and Aachen Day-Night show that GeoMix sets a new state of the art among descriptor-free methods, reducing 75th-percentile rotation error by 89\% and translation error by up to 90\% over the previous best, while generalizing zero-shot to unseen detectors and narrowing the gap to descriptor-based pipelines. Code is available at $\href{https://github.com/YejunZhang/Geomix}{\text{this links}}$.

</details>


### [122] [Towards Robustness against Typographic Attack with Training-free Concept Localization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02494)
*Bohan Liu,Wenqian Ye,Guangzhi Xiong,Zhenghao He,Sanchit Sinha,Aidong Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文发现CLIP视觉编码器在面对图像中的无关文字时会产生语义偏差（即印刷攻击），并提出一种无需训练的机制可解释性方法，通过采样解释隐藏状态表征、量化注意力头的语义/词汇关注度、挖掘电路机制，并通过对识别出的电路进行简单干预（如选择性调整注意力权重）来显著提升对印刷攻击的鲁棒性，在多个LVLM上验证了有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: CLIP模型作为大多数现代大型视觉语言模型的基础视觉编码器，存在一个关键但未被充分探索的缺陷：图像中的无关文字会干扰视觉表征，使其偏向词汇意义而非真实视觉语义，这种鲁棒性问题（印刷攻击）对安全关键应用（如自动驾驶）构成重大风险。

Method: 提出一种无需训练的机制可解释性方法：(1) 基于采样的隐藏状态表征解释；(2) 定量归因注意力头对语义与词汇的关注度；(3) 通过概率分析和电路挖掘，分离出Vision Transformer中过度编码词汇信息的组件；(4) 对识别出的电路直接施加简单干预（如选择性调整注意力权重），无需额外训练即可提升鲁棒性。

Result: 所提干预方法在目标分类任务上优于有监督和无训练的防御方法；应用于多个SOTA LVLM的视觉编码器后，在RIO-Bench上显著提升了印刷攻击干扰下的视觉问答准确率。

Conclusion: 该方法验证了通过机制可解释性定位词汇编码电路并进行直接干预，可以有效且泛化地提升CLIP及LVLM对印刷攻击的鲁棒性。

Abstract: Models trained via Contrastive Language-Image Pretraining (CLIP) serve as the foundational vision encoders for most modern Large Vision Language Models (LVLMs). Despite their widespread adoption, CLIP models exhibit a critical yet underexplored failure mode: irrelevant text appearing within images confounds visual representations, biasing them toward lexical meaning rather than true visual semantics. This robustness issue, commonly described as a Typographic Attack (TA), exposes a vulnerability that poses a significant risk to safety-critical applications such as autonomous driving. To achieve interpretable and effective robustness against TA, we propose a novel, training-free mechanistic interpretability method. Our method provides sampling-based interpretations of hidden state representations and quantitatively attributes semantic versus lexical focus to individual attention heads. Through probabilistic analysis and circuit mining, we isolate specific Vision Transformer (ViT) components that disproportionately encode lexical information, thereby identifying the mechanistic source of TA. We further show that simple interventions applied directly to the identified circuits, without any additional training, can substantially improve robustness against Typographic Attacks in object classification. These interventions, such as selective adjustment of attention weights, also outperform both supervised and training-free defense methods. Our experiments demonstrate that applying the proposed intervention to the vision encoders of several state-of-the-art LVLMs yields substantial gains in Visual Question Answering accuracy under Typographic Attack interference on RIO-Bench. These results confirm both the efficacy and the generalizability of our mechanistic approach. Code is released at https://github.com/Liu-524/SamplingTAR.

</details>


### [123] [Seek to Segment: Active Perception for Panoramic Referring Segmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02497)
*Song Tang,Shuming Hu,Xincheng Shuai,Henghui Ding,Yu-Gang Jiang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出主动全景指代分割（APRS）任务，让智能体在360°环境中通过调整视角主动搜索并分割用户指令指定的目标。提出PanoSeeker代理，利用视觉语言模型与EgoSphere空间视觉记忆，结合专家轨迹监督微调与强化学习后训练，实现高效非冗余的搜索与精准分割，在基准测试中显著优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有指代分割模型只能处理固定视角的静态图像，无法应用于需要智能体在连续360°环境中主动感知的具身AI场景，因此需要引入主动全景指代分割（APRS）任务来弥合这一差距。

Method: 提出PanoSeeker，包含：(1) EgoSphere显式空间视觉记忆模块，将局部观测逐步整合为统一的360°全景表示；(2) 视觉语言模型（VLM）驱动，基于记忆规划高效非冗余搜索轨迹；(3) 专家标注的搜索轨迹数据集用于监督微调；(4) 强化学习后训练以优化探索效率。智能体找到目标后执行主动视角对齐并输出分割掩码。

Result: 在建立的APRS基准测试上，PanoSeeker在搜索效率和分割精度上均显著优于适配后的最先进基线方法。

Conclusion: PanoSeeker通过记忆增强的主动感知策略有效解决了360°环境下的指代分割问题，为具身AI中的主动视觉感知提供了新的解决方案。

Abstract: Existing referring segmentation models passively process static images captured from fixed perspectives, limiting their applicability in Embodied AI, where agents must perform active perception in the continuous 360$^\circ$ environments. To bridge this gap, we introduce a novel task: Active Panoramic Referring Segmentation (APRS). In this setting, an agent is required to adjust its viewing direction ($Δθ, Δφ$) to explore the 360$^\circ$ environment, seeking the object specified by a user instruction for segmentation. To tackle this challenging task, we propose PanoSeeker, a memory-augmented agent for efficient APRS. Rather than relying on heuristic scanning, PanoSeeker integrates a Vision-Language Model (VLM) with EgoSphere, an explicit spatial visual memory. By progressively integrating sequential local observations into a unified 360$^\circ$ representation, EgoSphere enables the agent to plan efficient and non-redundant search trajectories. Once the target is found, the agent performs active viewpoint alignment and outputs the segmentation mask. Furthermore, we curate an expert-annotated search trajectory dataset with memory timelines for Supervised Fine-Tuning, followed by Reinforcement Learning post-training to explicitly optimize PanoSeeker's exploration efficiency. Extensive experiments on our newly established APRS benchmark demonstrate that PanoSeeker achieves superior search efficiency and segmentation accuracy, significantly outperforming adapted state-of-the-art baselines.

</details>


### [124] [From SRA to Self-Flow: Data Augmentation or Self-Supervision?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02508)
*Dengyang Jiang,Mengmeng Wang,Harry Yang,Jingdong Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文质疑了Self-Flow中双时间步调度（dual-time scheduling）的改进机制，通过Attention Separation实验发现，阻止不同噪声层级token间的注意力交互并不会降低性能，表明改进主要来自数据增强而非token间交互。同时，Attention Separation本身通过将单图像分割为多个有效训练部分来提供增强效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究从SRA到Self-Flow的改进机制，澄清双时间步调度的真正作用——是来自不同噪声层级token间的交互，还是来自噪声维度的数据增强。

Method: 提出Attention Separation方法，在保持与Self-Flow相同的双时间步输入的同时，阻断不同噪声层级token之间的注意力交互，通过消融实验分离token交互与数据增强两种因素。

Result: 移除token间交互不会降低性能甚至可能提升，表明Self-Flow的改进主要来自数据增强；Attention Separation本身通过分割图像提供数据增强效果。

Conclusion: 自表征对齐与双时间步及注意力分离增强相结合的设计在ImageNet上有效，揭示了Self-Flow改进的真正来源是数据增强而非token交互。

Abstract: Representation alignment has become an effective way to accelerate diffusion transformer training and improve generation quality. Recent self-alignment methods, such as SRA and Self-Flow, further remove the dependency on external pretrained encoders by constructing alignment within the diffusion model itself. However, the mechanism behind the improvement from SRA to Self-Flow, dual-time scheduling, remains under-examined: Self-Flow attributes its gain to interactions between tokens at different noise levels, where cleaner tokens help infer noisier ones. In this work, we revisit this explanation and ask whether the gain instead comes from data augmentation along the noise dimension. To disentangle these factors, we introduce Attention Separation, which preserves the same dual-timestep input as Self-Flow while blocking attention between tokens assigned to different noise levels. Surprisingly, removing such interaction does not degrade performance and can even improve it, suggesting that the improvement from SRA to Self-Flow mainly comes from data augmentation. Furthermore,We show that Attention Separation itself provides an augmentation effect by splitting a single image into multiple effective training parts to expand the training data. Based on these observations, we combine self-representation alignment with dual-timestep and attention-separation augmentation, and demonstrate the effectiveness of this design on ImageNet.

</details>


### [125] [PointDiT: Pixel-Space Diffusion for Monocular Geometry Estimation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02515)
*Haofei Xu,Rundi Wu,Philipp Henzler,Nikolai Kalischek,Michael Oechsle,Fabian Manhardt,Marc Pollefeys,Andreas Geiger,Federico Tombari,Michael Niemeyer*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出了一种极简的像素空间扩散Transformer，基于普通ViT架构，直接操作原始3D点图块，无需复杂的混合架构或几何潜在压缩，在单图像3D重建上超越了现有潜在扩散模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有单图像3D重建方法依赖复杂的混合架构和损失函数，或将几何压缩到潜在空间以利用预训练潜在扩散模型，这些架构开销和复杂损失设计是不必要的。

Method: 使用基于普通ViT的像素空间扩散Transformer，直接在原始3D点图块上操作，以预训练DINOv3的图像token为条件，从头训练扩散骨干，无需点图分词器。

Result: 超越了复杂的基于潜在扩散的模型，同时比混合替代方案更简单；产生更清晰的几何结构，在透明物体等高模糊区域更鲁棒。

Conclusion: 证明了无需混合架构、复杂损失或潜在空间压缩，极简的像素空间扩散方法即可实现更优的单图像3D重建性能。

Abstract: State-of-the-art single-image 3D reconstruction methods often rely on complex hybrid architectures and loss functions, or compress geometry into latent spaces in order to leverage pre-trained latent diffusion models. In this work, we show that such architectural overhead and intricate loss formulations are unnecessary. We introduce a minimalist pixel-space Diffusion Transformer, built on a plain ViT, that operates directly on raw 3D point map patches and is conditioned on image tokens from a pre-trained DINOv3. Unlike existing latent diffusion approaches, we train our diffusion backbone entirely from scratch, eliminating the need for point map tokenizers. Despite its simplicity, our approach surpasses complex latent-based diffusion models while remaining significantly simpler than hybrid alternatives. Notably, it produces sharper geometric structure and is more robust in highly ambiguous regions, such as transparent objects.

</details>


### [126] [Alignment Is All You Need For X-to-4D Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02516)
*Qiaowei Miao,Kehan Li,Yawei Luo,Yi Yang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出Align4D框架，通过目标距离对齐、运动-几何联合对齐和异步优化三大技术，实现任意模态输入到4D内容的高质量生成，并构建了X4D基准数据集。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 由于构建多样化数据集的成本高昂以及现有方法的可扩展性有限，任意用户定义模态到4D（X-to-4D）生成仍然具有挑战性。

Method: (1) 目标距离对齐：搜索视频对齐和多视角对齐的目标距离，协调4D渲染与视频及多视角扩散模型的先验；(2) 运动-几何联合对齐：通过同步视频和3D输入约束已知和未知视图，确保4D生成一致性；(3) 异步优化：解耦高斯属性和变形网络训练，提升运动和几何保真度。同时构建了X4D数据集。

Result: 在X4D和Consistent4D数据集上，Align4D在X-to-4D生成任务中达到了最先进的质量和一致性。

Conclusion: Align4D是一种灵活的框架，能够将任意模态输入转换为连贯的视频-3D对，利用视频引导4D运动、3D数据塑造4D几何，实现了高质量的任意模态到4D生成。

Abstract: Generative diffusion models excel at synthesizing high-quality images, videos, and 3D content under multimodal control. However, arbitrary user-defined modality-to-4D (X-to-4D) generation remains challenging due to the high cost of constructing diverse datasets and the limited scalability of existing methods. This paper presents Align4D, a flexible framework that translates any-modal input into coherent video-3D pairs, using video to guide 4D motion and 3D data to shape 4D geometry. Align4D introduces three key techniques: (1) Object Distance Alignment, which searches Video-Aligned and Multiview-Aligned Object Distances (VAOD/MAOD), respectively, to reconcile 4D renderings with video and the priors of multiview diffusion models; (2) Motion-Geometry Joint Alignment, which constrains known and unknown views through synchronized video and 3D inputs, ensuring consistent 4D generation; and (3) Asynchronous Optimization, which decouples Gaussian attribute and deformation network training to enhance motion and geometry fidelity. We further propose the X4D dataset, which integrates prompt, image, video, and 3D data for benchmarking. Experiments on X4D and Consistent4D demonstrate that Align4D achieves state-of-the-art quality and consistency in X-to-4D generation. Project page: https://miaoqiaowei.github.io/Align4D/.

</details>


### [127] [WorldDirector: Building Controllable World Simulators with Persistent Dynamic Memory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02517)
*Hanlin Wang,Hao Ouyang,Qiuyu Wang,Wen Wang,Qingyan Bai,Ka Leong Cheng,Yue Yu,Yixuan Li,Yihao Meng,Zichen Liu,Yanhong Zeng,Yujun Shen,Qifeng Chen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: WorldDirector提出了一种将语义运动编排与视觉生成显式解耦的视频世界模型框架，通过LLM协调3D轨迹与相机运动，实现动态对象持久记忆和自由视点探索，即使在对象长时间离开视野后重新进入时仍能保持精确的视觉身份。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有世界模型将物理动态与像素渲染纠缠在一起，依赖连续视觉观察来维持运动，导致动态对象在长时间离开视野后无法保持视觉一致性。

Method: 显式解耦语义运动编排与视觉生成，利用LLM协调3D轨迹与相机运动，将编排后的轨迹作为控制信号输入视频生成模型。

Result: 支持合成复杂且长时间的事件，具有前所未有的可控性和持久动态对象记忆，能够保持动态实体在重新进入场景时的精确视觉身份。

Conclusion: WorldDirector通过解耦运动编排与视觉生成，有效解决了动态对象长期离场后的视觉身份保持问题，实现了高度可控的视频世界模型。

Abstract: We present WorldDirector, a highly controllable video world model framework designed for persistent dynamic object memory and unrestricted viewpoint exploration. Unlike existing world models that entangle physical dynamics with pixel rendering and rely on continuous visual observation to sustain motion, our framework explicitly decouples semantic motion orchestration from visual generation. By leveraging an LLM to coordinate 3D trajectories with camera movements and subsequently employing these orchestrated trajectories as control signals for video generation, our approach ensures strict physical logic and appearance stability, successfully preserving the exact visual identities of dynamic entities even when they re-enter the scene after prolonged periods out of view. Experimental results demonstrate that our method supports the synthesis of complex and extended events with unprecedented controllability and persistent dynamic object memory. Project Page: https://worlddirector.github.io/

</details>


<div id='cs.AI'></div>

# cs.AI [[Back]](#toc)

### [128] [PACE: A Neuro-Symbolic Framework for Plausible and Actionable Counterfactual Explanations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01306)
*Pavel Iakovets,Liyanapathiranage Sudeepika Wajirakumari Samarathunga,Martin Thomas Horsch,Fadi Al Machot*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出PACE框架，一种模块化神经符号方法，通过结合神经预测模型与符号推理（ASP规则）来生成符合领域知识约束的可行反事实解释，并在Adult Income数据集上验证了其有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有反事实解释方法虽能生成改变预测结果的输入变化，但缺乏显式机制来融入领域知识和干预约束，导致产生不现实或不可行的推荐。

Method: 将预测与推理分离为两个组件：神经预测模型（分类器）和符号推理层（ASP规则编码领域约束），显式建模可行干预，模型无关且可适配不同领域。

Result: 在Adult Income数据集上，结合多层感知机分类器与ASP规则（编码教育、职业、工作时间的可行修改及不可变属性），结果表明符号约束能生成更满足领域可行性要求的解释，揭示了反事实有效性与合理性之间的权衡。

Conclusion: 神经符号方法在生成透明、可行性感知的反事实解释方面具有潜力，为可解释AI中实现符合领域知识的可操作解释提供了有效方向。

Abstract: Counterfactual explanations explain machine learning predictions by identifying minimal input changes that would alter a model's decision. Although many existing methods successfully generate prediction-changing alternatives, they often produce unrealistic or infeasible recommendations due to a lack of explicit mechanisms for incorporating domain knowledge and intervention constraints. Neuro-symbolic AI offers a promising direction by combining data-driven predictive models with symbolic reasoning capable of representing human-understandable rules and feasible actions. This paper presents PACE, a modular neuro-symbolic framework for generating feasibility-aware counterfactual explanations. The framework separates prediction and reasoning into two components: a neural predictive model for classification and a symbolic reasoning layer that enforces domain-specific constraints during counterfactual generation. By explicitly modeling feasible interventions, the framework produces explanations consistent with domain knowledge while remaining interpretable and actionable. The approach is model-agnostic and adaptable to domains requiring realistic decision support. A case study is conducted on the Adult Income dataset, combining a multilayer perceptron classifier with Answer Set Programming (ASP) rules encoding feasible modifications to education, occupation, and working hours while preserving immutable attributes. Results highlight the trade-off between counterfactual validity and plausibility and show that symbolic constraints yield explanations that better satisfy domain-specific feasibility requirements, illustrating the potential of neuro-symbolic methods for transparent, feasibility-aware counterfactual explanation in explainable AI.

</details>


### [129] [Auto-FL-Research: Agentic Search for Federated Learning Algorithms](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01366)
*Holger R. Roth,Ziyue Xu,Chester Chen,Daguang Xu,Peter Cnudde,Andrew Feng*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出Auto-FL-Research (AFR)，一个基于约束编码代理的工作流，用于自动搜索联邦学习算法配方（包括优化器、聚合规则、本地训练策略等）。在11个联邦学习任务上的评估显示，AFR能在部分任务上取得提升，但也暴露了种子敏感性和伪发现等问题，揭示了自动化搜索中需区分真正的FL机制改进、固定表面调参效应和单次运行伪迹。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 联邦学习研究涉及大量细碎但重要的算法选择（优化器、聚合规则、本地训练策略、正则化等），人工探索成本高昂且难以公平比较不同候选方案。现有工作缺乏自动化、系统化的方法来搜索和评估FL算法配方。

Method: 提出AFR框架，采用约束编码代理工作流：智能体可提出并实现候选训练算法（包括服务端聚合规则、客户端更新调度、本地目标和注册模型变体），而任务配置文件固定变异表面、计算预算、通信契约和最终模型评估。每个campaign记录候选得分、运行时间、编辑文件、工件和失败状态。

Result: 在5个FLamby医疗跨孤岛任务和6个LEAF任务上评估，5种子重复验证显示AFR在4个FLamby任务和5个LEAF任务上取得提升，同时暴露了种子敏感性和搜索选择的失败案例。同预算对照实验表明，部分提升来自真正的FL配方变化，另一些则是固定表面标量调参效应或在重复/留出评估中失败。

Conclusion: 混合结果本身是贡献：展示了如何将智能体生成的候选方案区分为真正的重复FL机制改进、固定表面调参效应和选择的单次运行伪迹。自动化FL算法搜索需要谨慎区分这三种来源，以避免虚假发现。

Abstract: Federated learning (FL) research often depends on many small but consequential algorithmic choices: optimizer variants, server aggregation rules, local training schedules, normalization, regularization, and model architecture. These choices are expensive to explore manually and difficult to compare fairly when candidate changes can also alter the FL training or evaluation path. In this work, we present Auto-FL-Research (AFR), a constrained coding-agent workflow for FL algorithmic recipe search. Agents may propose and implement candidate training algorithms, including server aggregation rules, client update schedules, local objectives, and registered model variants, while task profiles fix the mutation surface, compute budget, communication contract, and final model evaluation. Each campaign records candidate scores, runtime, edited files, artifacts, and failure status. We evaluate AFR on five healthcare cross-silo FLamby tasks and on grouped-client profiles for the five fixed LEAF datasets plus the LEAF synthetic task. Five-seed repeat evaluations support gains on four FLamby tasks and five of six LEAF profiles, while also exposing seed-sensitive and search-selected failure cases. Same-budget controls show that several gains correspond to FL-recipe changes, whereas other improvements are recovered by fixed-surface scalar controls or fail under repeat or held-out evaluation. These mixed outcomes are part of the contribution: they show how agent-generated candidates can be separated into repeated FL mechanisms, fixed-surface tuning effects, and selected single-run artifacts.

</details>


### [130] [The Wiola Architecture for Efficient Small Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01394)
*Aryuemaan Kumar Chowdhury,Afreen Shaik,Yaparla Bhargavi,Brahma Kumar*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出Wiola，一个完全原创的小型语言模型架构，包含五种独立新颖的组件：螺旋旋转位置编码(SRPE)、门控跨层注意力(GCLA)、自适应令牌合并(ATM)、双流前馈网络(DSFF)和WiolaRMSNorm。该架构与GPT、LLaMA等现有模型无结构关联，发布四种参数规模(120M-1.5B)，与HuggingFace生态兼容。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有语言模型架构(如GPT、LLaMA、Mistral)结构趋同，创新空间有限。作者希望从零开始构建一个完全原创的SLM架构，引入多种独立新颖的组件，探索超越现有范式的新设计路径。

Method: Wiola包含五个核心创新组件：(1) SRPE:在三维螺旋流形上嵌入位置，融合绝对、相对和层级位置信号；(2) GCLA:每层解码器可软交叉关注前两层的压缩摘要，增强层间连贯性；(3) ATM:在中间层动态合并语义冗余的相邻令牌，降低注意力复杂度；(4) DSFF:双并行流前馈网络，通过可学习逐维度门控融合；(5) WiolaRMSNorm:引入可学习偏移向量防止表示坍塌。

Result: 发布了四种参数规模(120M、360M、700M、1.5B)的模型，与GPT-2、LLaMA-2、Mistral进行了系统比较，完成了完整的复杂度分析和22项架构单元测试，与HuggingFace Transformers生态完全兼容。

Conclusion: Wiola展示了一种从零构建、完全原创的SLM架构的可行性，五种创新组件可独立或组合使用，为语言模型架构设计提供了新的方向。该工作开源并兼容主流生态，便于社区复现和扩展。

Abstract: We present Wiola, a fully original Small Language Model (SLM) architecture built from first principles, sharing no structural lineage with any existing model family including GPT, LLaMA, Mistral, or Falcon. Wiola introduces five independently novel components: (i) Spiral Rotary Positional Encoding (SRPE), which embeds token positions on a three-dimensional helical manifold combining absolute, relative, and hierarchical positional signals; (ii) Gated Cross-Layer Attention (GCLA), providing each decoder layer with soft cross-attention access to compressed summaries of two preceding layers for inter-layer coherence; (iii) Adaptive Token Merging (ATM), which dynamically merges se mantically redundant adjacent tokens in middle network layers to reduce attention complexity without information loss; (iv) Dual Stream Feed-Forward (DSFF), replacing the conventional MLP with two parallel streams fused by a learned per-dimension gate; and (v) WiolaRMSNorm, a modified normalisation introducing a per-dimension learned offset vector that prevents representation collapse. We provide complete mathematical derivations, architectural block diagrams, complexity analyses, and systematic comparisons against GPT-2, LLaMA-2, and Mistral. Wiola is released in four sizes (120M, 360M, 700M, and 1.5B parameters) and is fully compatible with the HuggingFace Transformers ecosystem, with all 22 architectural unit tests passing.

</details>


### [131] [Agent4cs: A Multi-agent System for Code Summarization in Large Hierarchical Codebases](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01425)
*Yongjian Tang,Ezgi Sarikayak,Doruk Tuncel,Jie M. Zhang,Thomas Runkler*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: Agent4cs 是一个多智能体框架，通过自底向上的方式对大型代码库进行摘要生成，包含摘要生成、关键词提取和质量保证三个智能体，在7个前沿模型上相比结构提示基线方法，语义一致性平均提升8%，关键词覆盖率最高提升38%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有代码摘要方法依赖单一语言模型或编码助手（如Claude Code），将源代码视为纯文本处理，未能充分利用代码库中丰富的相互依赖关系和层次结构信息，难以应对大型、复杂、结构混淆且文档不完整的代码库。

Method: 提出Agent4cs多智能体框架，采用自底向上（bottom-up）方式：摘要生成智能体负责生成稳健的摘要；关键词提取智能体主动识别子文件夹中的关键信息；质量保证智能体迭代优化输出的可读性、连贯性和完整性。

Result: 在7个前沿模型上，相比两种带代码片段的结构提示基线方法，所有文件夹层级的语义一致性平均提升8%；在真实数据集上，归一化关键词覆盖率最高提升38%。

Conclusion: Agent4cs通过多智能体协作和自底向上的层次化摘要策略，有效提升了大型代码库摘要的语义一致性和关键词覆盖率，验证了利用代码库结构信息进行多智能体协作摘要的可行性和有效性。

Abstract: Understanding large, complex codebases, especially those with obfuscated structures and incomplete documentation, remains a significant challenge. Existing code summarization solutions often rely on a single language model or coding assistant like Claude Code, and treat source code as flat text, underutilizing the rich interdependencies and hierarchical information within a repository. To address these shortcomings, we propose Agent4cs - a multi-agent framework that summarizes large codebases in a bottom-up fashion, where a summarization agent focuses on producing robust summaries; a keyword-extraction agent proactively identifies critical information from subfolders; and a quality-assurance agent iteratively refines the outputs for readability, coherence, and completeness. Evaluated on 7 frontier models, Agent4cs improves semantic consistency across all folder levels by average 8% compared to two structured prompting baselines with code segments. Furthermore, extensive evaluation on real-world datasets demonstrates up to 38% gains in normalized keyword coverage rate over the same baselines.

</details>


### [132] [When Should Service Agents Reconsider? Difficulty-Routed Control in Customer-Service Operations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01426)
*Qian Chen,Chengyuan Liu,Xin Yu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种难度路由的服务控制架构，通过轻量级路由器将常规会话保留在低成本基线路径上，将操作耦合的会话路由到升级工作流，该工作流使用冲突感知通信和写入触发重新考虑，在不增加常规会话负担的前提下，在关键后端写入前集中安全防护。在零售和航空领域的人验证数据集上验证了该方法能有效提升操作冲突场景的可靠性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自主客服代理正从对话界面转向执行操作（如退款、取消、修改订单等），这一转变带来了服务控制问题：企业需要保持常规服务快速低摩擦，同时防止操作错误。现有的统一控制方法无法在效率与安全性之间取得平衡。

Method: 提出难度路由的服务控制架构：1) 轻量级路由器区分常规会话与操作耦合会话；2) 常规会话走低成本基线路径；3) 升级路径采用冲突感知通信和写入触发重新考虑机制，在关键后端写入前集中推理和安全防护。

Result: 在零售任务中，该方法在操作冲突的服务请求上持续提升了可靠性。路由证据显示更强的控制被定向到冲突请求而非常规请求。对话和工具使用分析表明改进来自证据收集、写入分离和写入前重新考虑，而非无差别扩展交互或工具链。航空任务验证了相同的服务控制逻辑。

Conclusion: 难度路由的服务控制架构能有效解决自主客服从对话转向操作执行带来的服务控制挑战，在不影响常规会话效率的前提下，针对性增强操作耦合场景的安全保障，具有跨领域的泛化能力。

Abstract: Autonomous customer-service agents are shifting from conversational interfaces toward operational execution roles: they retrieve firm records, apply service policies, and execute backend writes such as refunds, cancellations, exchanges, order modifications, and reservation changes. This shift creates a service-control problem: firms must keep routine service fast and low-friction while preventing operational errors on requests where customer instructions, policy constraints, firm records, and backend writes interact. We propose a difficulty-routed service-control architecture that asks when service agents should reconsider before acting. A lightweight router keeps routine sessions on a low-cost baseline path and routes operationally coupled sessions to an escalated workflow. The escalated path uses conflict-aware communication and write-triggered reconsideration to concentrate deliberation and safeguards before consequential backend writes, rather than applying additional control uniformly across all service sessions. We evaluate the architecture on human-verified retail and airline tasks from $τ^{2}$-bench. In retail, the method improves reliability consistently on service requests with operational conflict. Routing evidence shows that stronger control is directed toward conflicted requests rather than broadly applied to routine ones. Dialogue and tool-use profiles suggest that gains do not come from indiscriminate interaction expansion or broader tool chains; instead, added turns and tool calls support evidence gathering, write separation, and pre-write reconsideration. Case-level evidence shows that the escalated workflow preserves fallback plans, binds retrieved records to the correct action, sequences writes, and decomposes multi-entity requests. Airline results extend the same service-control logic to reservation operations.

</details>


### [133] [CreativityNeuro: Steering Language Model Weights to Improve Divergent Thinking and Reduce Mode Collapse](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01433)
*Samuel Schapiro,Core Francisco Park,Felix Sosa,Lav R. Varshney*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出CreativityNeuro，一种无需数据、无需重训练或梯度微调，通过对比权重引导增强大模型发散思维的方法。在多项创造力评估中，该方法显著提升原创性、惊喜度和创造力，并有效减少模式坍塌，且在更开放的长文本任务中具有良好泛化能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型在开放性问题中倾向于生成相似回答，即"人工蜂群效应"，这限制了LLM在创造性领域的应用。现有方法依赖行为数据、重训练或梯度微调，成本高且泛化性有限。本文旨在开发一种轻量级、无需数据的方法来增强LLM的发散思维。

Method: CreativityNeuro采用对比权重引导（contrastive weight steering）技术，通过在权重空间而非激活空间进行方向性调整，引导模型远离常见的、趋同的生成模式，从而增强发散思维。该方法无需训练数据、重训练或基于梯度的微调。

Result: 在Divergent Association Task (DAT)上提升高达14个百分位点；在720人参与的大规模人工评估中，Alternative Uses Test (AUT)和Task Task任务在原创性、惊喜度和创造力上均有显著提升；所有三项任务中模式坍塌均显著减少；激活引导在DAT上表现相当但无法泛化到AUT和Task Task，而权重引导方法具备跨任务泛化能力。

Conclusion: CreativityNeuro能有效增强LLM的发散思维并减少模式坍塌，且无需行为数据、重训练或梯度微调，为提升LLM在创造性领域的表现提供了一种简单直接的方法。权重空间引导相比激活空间引导具有更好的任务泛化性。

Abstract: Divergent thinking is a crucial aspect of creativity, yet large language models (LLMs) tend to consistently generate similar responses to open-ended questions, in what has been termed the artificial hivemind effect. Here, we introduce CreativityNeuro, a data-free method for enhancing divergent thinking in LLMs via contrastive weight steering. We evaluate our method across multiple creativity assessments and report several main findings. On the Divergent Association Task (DAT), a vocabulary-space creativity test, CreativityNeuro improves performance by up to 14 human percentile points. Next, in a large-scale human evaluation (N=720) on the Alternative Uses Test (AUT) and the Task Task, CreativityNeuro achieves significant improvements in originality, surprise, and creativity, transferring to longer-form and more open-ended tasks. Importantly, we find that across all three tasks, CreativityNeuro demonstrably reduces measures of mode collapse. Moreover, activation steering achieves comparable performance to CreativityNeuro on the DAT, but it does not transfer to the AUT and Task Task, demonstrating the effectiveness of weight-space steering in generalizing to unseen tasks. In conclusion, CreativityNeuro improves divergent thinking and reduces mode collapse without requiring behavioral data, re-training, or gradient-based fine-tuning, providing a straightforward way to enhance LLM performance in creative domains.

</details>


### [134] [Discrete Diffusion Language Models for Interactive Radiology Report Drafting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01436)
*Max Van Puyvelde,Halil Ibrahim Gulluk,Wim Van Criekinge,Olivier Gevaert*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文展示了扩散语言模型（DiffusionGemma-26B）在医学视觉问答任务上与其同尺寸自回归模型（Gemma-4-26B）性能持平甚至更优，解码速度快3.5-4.4倍，并具备自回归模型缺乏的任意顺序填充能力，适合处理真实医疗报告中不完整或不一致的文本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 医学基础模型目前几乎全部采用自回归架构，而扩散语言模型在文本生成领域已展现出竞争力，但在医学场景中的潜力尚未被探索。本文旨在验证扩散模型能否在医学视觉问答任务中匹敌甚至超越自回归模型，并挖掘其独特优势。

Method: 将混合专家扩散语言模型DiffusionGemma-26B与同尺寸的自回归模型Gemma-4-26B在医学视觉问答数据集上进行对比，两者均使用相同的LoRA微调方案，采用对冗余不敏感的LLM裁判进行评分。

Result: 扩散模型在所有医学视觉问答数据集上均持平或超越自回归模型；微调后的模型（3.8B活跃参数）与前沿视觉语言模型竞争力相当；解码速度提升3.5-4.4倍；扩散模型具备自回归模型所缺乏的任意顺序填充能力。

Conclusion: 扩散语言模型在医学视觉问答任务中不仅性能不逊于自回归模型，还拥有更快的解码速度和独特的任意顺序填充能力，更适合处理真实医疗报告中文本不完整或不一致的情况，具有临床应用潜力。

Abstract: Diffusion language models, which generate text by denoising a token canvas bidirectionally instead of emitting tokens left to right, have become competitive with autoregressive (AR) generation. Medical foundation models, however, remain almost entirely autoregressive. We adapt a mixture-of-experts diffusion language model, DiffusionGemma-26B, and benchmark it against its same-size AR sibling Gemma-4-26B under an identical LoRA recipe on medical visual question answering datasets, scored by a verbosity-robust LLM judge. Diffusion matches or exceeds AR on all of them, and the finetuned model (3.8B active) is competitive with frontier vision-language models; its decoding is also 3.5-4.4x faster. Beyond this parity, the diffusion model offers a drafting capability AR lacks: any-order infill. Because the canvas is denoised bidirectionally, a radiologist can fix report fragments and have the model fill the text between them, an operation inherent to diffusion but not to autoregression, which is subpar at it. This suits real reports, which are often terse or inconsistent across clinicians and institutions.

</details>


### [135] [Beyond Next-Token Prediction: An RLVR Proof of Concept for Tool-Use Agents on Atlassian Workflows](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01465)
*Karthikeya Aditya Vissa,Sankalp Mane,Ananya Mantravadi,Harshit Rajgarhia,Abhishek Mukherji*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文针对大语言模型在专有企业SaaS工作流中因“预测下一个token”与“执行API动作”目标不匹配而失败的问题，提出直接在目标环境中使用基于可验证奖励的强化学习（RLVR）来弥合差距。在五个高保真合成环境（模拟Jira REST v3和Confluence v2 API）上，以工具调用轨迹计算奖励（无需真实API、学习型评判器或人工标注），对Qwen3-1.7B和Qwen3.5-4B进行GRPO训练，在四个非退化场景中将平均奖励从0.35-0.92提升至0.95-1.00，最大提升为Confluence页面创建（0.35→1.00）。局限性：手工构建可验证奖励难以扩展，且一个场景（ticket-transition）奖励饱和，4B基线已满分。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM训练目标（预测下一个token）与执行企业API操作（正确调用端点、参数、顺序）之间存在目标不匹配，导致静默失败（遗漏必填字段、幻觉工具、过早停止）。现有方法依赖真实API、学习型评判器或人工标注，成本高且难以在专有场景中规模化。本文希望探索RLVR是否能在无需这些条件的情况下，直接在目标环境中优化小模型的企业API调用效果。

Method: 构建五个合成环境（模拟Jira REST v3和Confluence v2 API模式，保持模式保真度），奖励完全基于工具调用轨迹计算（无需真实API、学习型评判器或人工标签）。使用相同的奖励检查器驱动GRPO训练，对Qwen3-1.7B和Qwen3.5-4B进行RLVR训练，并与提示词基线对比。

Result: 在四个非退化场景中，RL训练的策略将平均奖励从4B基线的0.35-0.92提升至0.95-1.00；最大提升在Confluence页面创建场景（0.35→1.00）。一个场景（ticket-transition）奖励已饱和，4B基线已满分。

Conclusion: RLVR是使小模型在专有企业API场景中实现结果优化的可行初步方案，但手工构建可验证奖励难以规模化（仅适用于报告中少数端点），且部分场景基线已饱和。该方法可作为通往更大规模、更自动化奖励设计的中间步骤。

Abstract: Large language models are trained to predict the next token, not to act inside a specific API. In niche enterprise SaaS workflows -- where success means hitting the right endpoint with the right nested arguments in the right order -- this objective mismatch shows up as silent failures: dropped required fields, hallucinated tools, or early stops after a single read. We ask whether Reinforcement Learning with Verifiable Rewards (RLVR), applied directly in the target environment, closes the gap. As a proof of concept we build a suite of five synthetic environments emulating the Jira REST v3 and Confluence v2 APIs at schema fidelity; rewards are computed entirely from the tool-call trace, with no live API, no learned judge, and no human label in the loop. Scoring prompted Qwen3-1.7B and Qwen3.5-4B on the same checkers that drive GRPO training, we find that on the four scenarios whose rewards are non-degenerate the RL-trained policy lifts average reward from a 4B-baseline range of 0.35--0.92 to 0.95--1.00, with the largest single gain on Confluence page creation ($0.35 \rightarrow 1.00$). We position this as a preliminary step toward outcome-optimised small models for niche enterprise APIs, and foreground two limitations a workshop reader should weigh: hand-crafting verifiable rewards does not scale beyond the handful of endpoints reported here, and one of our five scenarios (ticket-transition) has a saturating reward shape that the prompted 4B already maxes out.

</details>


### [136] [World Feedback for Clinical Agents: Diagnosing RL in FHIR Environments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01470)
*Ananya Mantravadi,Harshit Rajgarhia,Prasanna Desikan,Abhishek Mukherji*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文通过构建MedAgentBench-v3基准，发现纯强化学习在临床协议执行任务中受限于能力天花板和格式知识壁垒，提出决策/格式知识/查找分类法来预测RL可学习性，并建议SFT注入知识、RL学习条件逻辑的混合方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 临床协议执行任务（如检查化验值、应用阈值、生成FHIR订单）天然适合用RL训练，但现有基准（MedAgentBench v1/v2）存在41.7%的静默完成上限，导致不作为成为RL主导策略。需要构建更合理的基准并系统分析RL在此类任务中的结构性障碍。

Method: 审计现有基准发现问题后构建MAB-v3（508个任务，8.9%上限）。使用Qwen3-8B进行训练，对比纯RL与基于规则的SFT表现，识别能力天花板（10/20任务类型零基础表现）和格式知识壁垒（3/20类型需要精确临床代码）。提出决策/格式知识/查找分类法预测RL可学习性。

Result: 纯RL达到18.2% pass@1，规则化SFT达到34.1%，差距15.9个百分点完全归因于上述两种壁垒。10/20任务类型基础表现为零（零梯度），3/20类型需要精确临床代码（RL无法通过探索发现）。

Conclusion: 纯RL不足以解决临床协议执行任务，需要结合SFT注入格式知识（如代码）和RL学习条件决策逻辑的分类混合方法。

Abstract: Clinical protocol-execution tasks -- checking a lab value, applying a threshold, placing a correctly structured FHIR order -- are natural candidates for RL from world feedback: once clinical SMEs encode decision logic into a verifier, that verifier grades unlimited rollouts without per-episode annotation. But applying RL requires a sound feedback channel and sufficient base capability. We audit MedAgentBench v1/v2, find a 41.7\% silent-finish ceiling that makes inaction the RL dominant strategy, and construct \textbf{MedAgentBench-v3 (MAB-v3)} (508 tasks, 8.9\% ceiling). Training Qwen3-8B exposes two structural barriers: a \emph{capability ceiling} (10/20 task types have 0\% base performance, zero gradient) and a \emph{format-knowledge barrier} (3/20 types require exact clinical codes undiscoverable by exploration). Pure RL reaches 18.2\% pass@1 vs.\ 34.1\% for rule-based SFT; the 15.9~pp gap is attributable entirely to these barriers. A decision/format-knowledge/lookup taxonomy predicts RL learnability and prescribes the fix: SFT to inject codes, RL to learn conditionals.

</details>


### [137] [Procedural Memory Distillation: Online Reflection for Self-Improving Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01480)
*Ye Liu,Srijan Bansal,Bo Pang,Yang Li,Zeyu Leo Liu,Yifei Ming,Zixuan Ke,Shafiq Joty,Semih Yavuz*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出过程记忆蒸馏（PMD），将跨回合的验证信号转化为可复用的过程记忆并蒸馏到策略权重中，使无记忆模型在推理时获得记忆能力，在SCI KNOW EVAL和LIVE CODE BENCH上比SDPO提升3.8-13.6%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有RLVR和SDPO等方法仅利用回合级的验证信号，丢弃了 rollout 中丰富的过程信息（如哪些策略持续通过验证、哪些失败模式反复出现），导致跨回合和跨epoch的交叉信号无法被捕获和复用。

Method: PMD构建三个层次的过程记忆（原始轨迹、自反思策略与教训、跨问题的高层行为模式），通过记忆条件化的自教师从积累经验中监督学生的rollout，实现策略与记忆的协同进化：策略生成rollout更新记忆，记忆塑造监督信号更新策略。

Result: 在Qwen3-8B和OLMo3-Instruct-7B上，PMD比SDPO在SCI KNOW EVAL提升3.8-5.5%，在LIVE CODE BENCH提升7.9-13.6%。冻结记忆或策略均导致性能下降超过10%。

Conclusion: 跨回合过程记忆的复用和协同进化机制是提升RLVR效果的关键，记忆与策略的相互更新比单向蒸馏更有效。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning with verifiable rewards (RLVR), along with recent selfdistillation variants such as SDPO, evaluates each rollout against a verifier and updates the policy from that episode-level signal. However, the richer procedural information in the rollout is rarely retained or reused. Across episodes and epochs, the model repeatedly encounters related problems under a changing policy, producing cross-episode signals that episode-local updates cannot capture: which strategies consistently pass verification, which failure modes persist, which patterns recur. We propose Procedural Memory Distillation (PMD), which converts these crossepisode signals into reusable procedural memory and distills it into the policy's weights during training. This memory functions as a training scaffold, absorbed into the policy itself, yielding a memory-free model at inference. PMD organizes the memory at three levels of abstraction: raw trajectories, self-reflected strategies and lessons, and higher-level behavioral patterns that recur across problems, all extracted online from the model's own trajectories. A memory-conditioned self-teacher draws on the accumulated experience to supervise the student on its own rollouts, enabling student to progressively internalize procedural knowledge within its parameters. The central design principle is co-evolution: the policy generates rollouts that update the memory, and memory shapes the supervision that updates the policy. Empirically, across Qwen3-8B and OLMo3-Instruct-7B, PMD improves over SDPO by 3.8-5.5% on SCIKNOWEVAL and 7.9-13.6% on LIVECODEBENCH. Co-evolution powers these gains: freezing either the memory or the policy trails PMD by more than 10% across SCIKNOWEVAL domains.

</details>


### [138] [The Agentic Garden of Forking Paths](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01507)
*Jiacheng Miao,Jonathan K Pritchard,James Zou*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文揭示AI代理能够捕捉人类研究者在数据分析中的隐性分歧路径，并通过不同角色设定重现甚至放大这些分歧。研究发现，42个人类团队分析同一移民数据集时，AI代理可复现72%的意识形态差异。尽管结论对立，86%的AI分析报告能通过独立AI审查，78%通过人类专家审查。作者提出m值（多重宇宙值）和Agentic Bootstrap方法，将单一分析结果置于合理分析分布的背景下评估科学可信度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 实证研究中不同分析选择常导致不同结论，但这些隐性分叉路径难以观察。作者希望利用AI代理使这些路径显式化，并量化单一分析结果在合理分析空间中的极端程度，从而解决选择性探索与报告的问题。

Method: 通过给AI代理分配不同角色设定（personas），让其从相同数据中进行分析；在四个高风险领域进行实验，并与42个人类研究团队的移民数据分析结果对比。提出m值（多重宇宙值）衡量分析路径结果的极端程度，并设计Agentic Bootstrap方法利用AI代理采样合理分析路径来估计m值。

Result: AI代理可复现72%的人类意识形态差异；86%的AI报告通过独立AI审查，78%通过多数人类专家审查；在人类移民研究中，13.5%的报告分析落在分析空间最极端的5%内（m<0.05）。

Conclusion: 科学证据评估不应仅依赖单一报告分析，而应考虑其在合理可报告分析分布中的位置。Agentic Bootstrap使这一分布可观察，并将其转化为科学可信度的标准。

Abstract: Empirical research rarely admits a unique analysis. Different analytical choices can lead to different conclusions from the same data, yet these hidden forking paths are difficult to observe. We show that AI agents capture much of the analytical variation among human researchers while making these paths explicit. Across four high-stakes domains, assigning different personas is sufficient for AI agents to report divergent, often opposing, conclusions from the same data and question, with findings systematically aligned with those beliefs. In a study in which 42 human research teams analyzed the same immigration dataset, AI agents reproduced 72% of the human ideological gap in reported effect estimates. Despite reaching opposing conclusions, it is difficult to identify clear issues in each analysis based on the final AI reports: 86% passed independent AI review and 78% passed majority human expert review. These findings suggest that the central challenge is often not flawed analyses, but selective exploration and reporting from a large space of methodologically defensible analyses. AI agents may amplify this longstanding problem by making such exploration inexpensive and scalable. To address this, we introduce the m-value (multiverse value), the probability that an analysis path would produce a claim at least as extreme as the reported one. We further introduce Agentic Bootstrap, which estimates the m-value by using AI agents to sample plausible analysis paths. Applied to the human immigration study, 13.5% of reported human analyses fell in the most extreme 5% of the analysis space (m<0.05). Scientific evidence should therefore be evaluated not only by a single reported analysis but also by its position within the distribution of analyses that could reasonably have been reported. Agentic Bootstrap makes this distribution observable and turns it into a criterion for scientific credibility.

</details>


### [139] [Janus: a Playground for User-Involved Agentic Permission Management](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01510)
*Natalie Grace Brigham,Eugene Bagdasarian,Tadayoshi Kohno,Franziska Roesner*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出Janus系统，用于探索和评估AI代理中用户参与的权限管理设计。通过实现六种权限助手并在三个场景下评估，发现用户输入对隐私安全至关重要，AI增强可减轻认知负担，但需要考虑用户疲劳等现实因素，且没有单一设计适用于所有情境。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI代理自主执行工具调用引发权限管理问题，用户应扮演什么角色尚未得到充分探索。

Method: 构建Janus系统（含Janus-Core模块化代理系统和Janus-Harness自动评估框架），基于概念模型实现六种权限助手，在三个场景和三个合成响应者上进行评估。

Result: 用户输入对增强隐私安全至关重要；AI增强用户决策可减少认知负担；需考虑用户疲劳等现实行为；不同设计在不同情境下表现各异，无单一最优方案。

Conclusion: 需要更原则性、上下文敏感的方法来部署代理系统中的权限助手，Janus已开源以支持未来研究。

Abstract: AI agents that autonomously execute tool calls on a user's behalf raise pressing questions about permission management: what role could users play, and what role should they play? Despite many proposed approaches, the user's role in agentic permission management remains under explored. We introduce Janus, a playground system for implementing and evaluating user-involved agentic permission management designs. Janus consists of two components: Janus-Core, a modular agentic system supporting a diverse spectrum of permission management designs, and Janus-Harness, an automated evaluation framework. Grounded in a conceptual model that identifies key design axes for user involvement, we implement six permission assistants spanning the design space and evaluate them across three scenarios and three synthetic responders. We demonstrate that user input is critical and can significantly strengthen privacy and security, that AI augmentation of user decisions can help reduce cognitive load, and that realistic user behavior including permission fatigue must be accounted for in system design. No single design performs optimally across all contexts, motivating a more principled and context-sensitive approach to deploying permission assistants in agentic systems. Janus is publicly available to support future investigation into this dimension of agentic system design.

</details>


### [140] [Revisiting Chain-of-Thought Reasoning under Limited Supervision: Semi-supervised Chain-of-Thought Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01511)
*Hongyang He,Jiuming Liu,Victor Sanchez*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出Semi-CoT框架，利用无标注问题构建伪推理监督信号，通过语义熵过滤低置信度推理链，实现半监督思维链学习。实验显示熵门控能选择高精度伪思维链，但在不同数据集上效果差异较大。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有思维链方法主要将推理链用作推理时的提示，而很少将生成的推理痕迹作为半监督学习信号重复利用，这限制了无标注数据的潜在价值。

Method: 提出Semi-CoT框架：对每个无标注问题采样多个伪思维链，通过估算答案级语义熵选择低熵（高置信度）推理链作为可靠的伪思维链示范，从而将思维链从推理时优化扩展到半监督伪监督。

Result: 熵门控能选出高精度伪思维链，伪答案精度达91.36%-100%；Semi-CoT在SVAMP和GSM8K上有小幅提升，AQuA出现负迁移，MultiArith达到性能上限。

Conclusion: 无标注问题能提供可靠的伪推理信号，但有效利用仍需更强的示范选择或学生训练策略。

Abstract: Chain-of-thought (CoT) reasoning has emerged as an effective approach for activating latent reasoning capabilities in large language models. However, most existing CoT methods use reasoning chains mainly as inference-time prompts, while the generated reasoning traces are rarely reused as semi-supervised learning signals. In this report, we define \textbf{Semi-supervised Chain-of-Thought Learning} and propose \textbf{Semi-CoT}, a simple framework that uses unlabeled questions to construct pseudo reasoning supervision. Semi-CoT samples multiple pseudo-CoTs for each unlabeled question, estimates answer-level semantic entropy, and selects low-entropy reasoning chains as reliable pseudo-CoT demonstrations. This extends the self-training view of CoT from inference-time refinement to semi-supervised pseudo-supervision. Pilot experiments on AQuA, SVAMP, GSM8K, and MultiArith show that the entropy gate selects high-precision pseudo-CoTs, with pseudo-answer precision ranging from $91.36\%$ to $100\%$. Semi-CoT also gives small gains on SVAMP and GSM8K, while AQuA shows negative transfer and MultiArith reaches a ceiling.
