Result: 在SWE-bench Lite上评估：OpenClaw总token减少51.5%，成本降低36.4%；Claude Code总token减少38.9%，成本降低27.3%。提交解决率仅从26.0%降至24.0%（OpenClaw），从32.0%降至30.0%（Claude Code）。

Conclusion: ContextSniper在显著降低token消耗和成本的同时，仅牺牲了少量修复性能，证明了其精确证据选择策略在仓库级程序修复中的有效性。

Abstract: Large language model agents can repair real repository issues, but they often spend large context budgets on whole-file reads, broad searches, and long terminal outputs where useful evidence is mixed with irrelevant code and logs. This paper presents ContextSniper, AntTrail's token-efficient code memory layer for repository-level program repair. As the coding specialization of AntTrail's broader agent memory engine, ContextSniper implements the Sniper feature for precision evidence selection: it retrieves candidate code and runtime evidence, ranks it with hybrid retrieval signals, filters long outputs through an intention-aware context gate, and returns compact evidence packets while preserving recoverable source context outside the prompt. We evaluate ContextSniper on SWE-bench Lite with OpenClaw and Claude Code, using 50 task runs per host-agent condition. ContextSniper reduces total token use by 51.5% and logged cost by 36.4% for OpenClaw, and reduces total token use by 38.9% and estimated cost by 27.3% for Claude Code. Submitted-resolution rates decrease slightly, from 26.0% to 24.0% for OpenClaw and from 32.0% to 30.0% for Claude Code. ContextSniper's pilot testing scripts are open-sourced at https://github.com/Calluking/ContextSniper

</details>


### [178] [ElephantAgent: Contextual State Continuity in Agentic Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01919)
*Jiankai Jin,Xiangzheng Zhang,Zhao Liu,Wenzhuo Xu,Dongdong Yang,Deyue Zhang,Quanchen Zou*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: ElephantAgent是一个通过上下文状态连续性协议来防御智能体系统上下文状态投毒攻击的框架，利用可信硬件复制和线性化账本实现状态完整性验证与历史可追溯性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有智能体系统因依赖外部工具和持久化内存而面临工具描述投毒和内存投毒等新型攻击，深层问题在于代理规划和执行过程中缺乏可验证的上下文状态连续性。

Method: 定义上下文状态为安全关键子集；在每个查询前重新计算本地上下文状态摘要并与授权摘要比对；利用复制可信硬件维护线性化账本记录授权状态转换；提供历史可追溯性以支持事后审计和恢复到已知安全状态。

Result: 能够检测带外状态篡改，并通过历史可追溯性处理带内语义滥用，支持恢复到已知良好的先前状态。

Conclusion: ElephantAgent将状态连续性保护从传统系统扩展到智能体系统的演化上下文状态，有效防御上下文状态投毒攻击。

Abstract: Agentic systems enhance their capabilities by invoking external tools and maintaining persistent memory. However, these external dependencies introduce novel attack surfaces. Recent tool and memory poisoning attacks show that maliciously crafted tool descriptors and poisoned memory can covertly bias agent behavior. These threats reflect a deeper issue: the lack of verifiable continuity in the agent's contextual state for planning and execution. We present ElephantAgent, a protocol that enforces Contextual State Continuity to defend against contextual state poisoning. Inspired by prior state-continuity mechanisms (e.g., Nimble), ElephantAgent extends this protection to the evolving contextual state of agentic systems. We define the contextual state as the bounded, security-critical subset of the agent's entire context (e.g., tool state and memory). Before processing each query, ElephantAgent recomputes the digest of the local contextual state and verifies it against the latest authorized digest. Using replicated trusted hardware, ElephantAgent maintains a linearizable ledger of authorized contextual state transitions and detects out-of-band state tampering. To handle in-band semantic abuse, ElephantAgent additionally provides Historical Traceability, enabling conditional post-hoc audit and recovery to a known-good prior state.

</details>


### [179] [A-TMA: Decoupling State-Aware Memory Failures in Long-Term Agent Memory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01935)
*Zitong Shi,Yixuan Tang,Anthony Kum Hoe Tung*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 针对LLM智能体长期记忆中的"幽灵记忆"问题（新旧事实混杂导致误导），提出状态感知覆盖层ATMA，从记忆库维护、检索和回答时消解三个层面优化，并在冲突密集型基准LTP上验证了有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM智能体作为持久助手需要长期记忆，但用户事实会变化。现有记忆系统中，旧事实、当前事实和转变事实混杂存储，检索时无法区分，导致回答模型被误导。这种"幽灵记忆"状态协调失败亟需解决。

Method: 提出ATMA（状态感知覆盖层），包含三个层面：1）记忆库层面：保留被取代的记录和转变记录；2）检索层面：为查询请求的状态视图构建证据包；3）回答层面：暴露当前、历史和转变标签给QA模型。同时构建了冲突密集型基准LTP用于评估。

Result: 在LTP基准上，Graphiti+ATMA相较Graphiti冲突准确率提升0.240。在LoCoMo上，Graphiti+ATMA将时间F1从0.0295提升至0.1705。

Conclusion: 明确的状态角色标注可以降低被最终QA准确率掩盖的记忆失败。记忆系统应从库维护、检索和回答三个层面解耦评估，以定位幽灵记忆的具体发生位置。

Abstract: Long term memory lets LLM agents act as persistent assistants, but user facts change. A useful memory system must know what is true now, what used to be true, and what changed. We study \emph{ghost memory}, a state coordination failure in which old, current, and transition facts coexist in the memory bank, remain mixed during retrieval, and mislead the answer model. We argue that memory systems should be understood and optimized from three levels: bank maintenance, retrieval, and answer time resolution. We propose ATMA, a state aware overlay for existing memory systems. ATMA keeps superseded and transition records in the bank, builds evidence packets for the query's requested state view, and exposes current, historical, and transition labels to QA. We further call for decoupled evaluation of bank, retrieval, and answer level failures, since final QA accuracy can hide where ghost memory occurs. To make this failure measurable, we build LTP (LoCoMo Temporal Plus), a conflict heavy benchmark for ghost memory, and evaluate on LoCoMo for long conversation generalization. On LTP, Graphiti+ATMA improves conflict accuracy by 0.240 absolute over Graphiti. On LoCoMo, Graphiti+ATMA raises temporal F1 from 0.0295 to 0.1705. The gains are host dependent, but they indicate that explicit state roles can reduce memory failures hidden by final QA accuracy.

</details>


### [180] [Atomic Task Graph: A Unified Framework for Agentic Planning and Execution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01942)
*Yue Zhang,Sihan Chen,Ziwen Huang,Hanyun Cui,Kangye Ji,Zhi Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出原子任务图(ATG)框架，通过显式图结构暴露子任务间依赖关系，实现规划可追溯、执行可并行、错误可定位修复，仅用7B-8B小模型即在多个交互基准上超越强基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于LLM的智能体方法依赖大模型或任务微调提升性能，前者计算成本高，后者泛化差；基于prompt的方法虽无需训练但隐式依赖导致中间结果难以复用。

Method: ATG维护显式有向无环图(DAG)结构：规划阶段递归分解高层任务为子任务DAG序列；执行阶段利用依赖关系并行执行独立分支；错误检测时通过图演化历史定位并局部修复受影响区域。

Result: 在三个交互式基准测试中，仅使用7B-8B参数量的基础模型，ATG在成功率和执行效率上均持续优于强基线方法。

Conclusion: 显式的图结构控制框架能让小模型在复杂多步任务中达到优异的规划、执行和容错能力，避免了扩大模型规模或任务特定微调的需求。

Abstract: LLM-based agents have shown strong potential for solving complex multi-step tasks, yet existing performance improvements often rely on either scaling to larger backbone models or task-specific fine-tuning. The former incurs substantial computational costs, while the latter typically generalizes poorly across different tasks. Although prompt-based control is training-free and broadly applicable, existing methods still leave input-output dependencies between subtasks implicit in textual trajectories, making verified intermediate results difficult to reuse. To address these limitations, we propose Atomic Task Graph (ATG), a unified control framework for planning and execution. Specifically, ATG maintains an explicit graph to expose dependencies and support reuse. During planning, it recursively decomposes a high-level task into subtasks, forming a sequence of directed acyclic graphs (DAGs) whose evolution can be traced. During execution, the dependencies exposed by ATG allow independent branches to be executed in parallel, thereby improving execution efficiency. When failures are detected, ATG leverages the graph evolution history to localize the error source and repair only the affected region, preserving validated regions unchanged. Experiments show that ATG consistently outperforms strong baselines in success rate and execution efficiency across three interactive benchmarks using only 7B-8B backbones.

</details>


### [181] [OntoLearner: A Modular Python Library for Ontology Learning with Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01977)
*Hamed Babaei Giglou,Jennifer D'Souza,Andrei Aioanei,Nandana Mihindukulasooriya,Sören Auer*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出OntoLearner框架，统一了本体访问、LLM驱动的学习流程和标准化基准测试，通过180个跨领域本体和22个检索模型与12个LLM的大规模实验，发现本体学习的主要瓶颈是模型知识编码与本体组织结构之间的结构性错配，而非模型能力本身。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 本体学习领域长期缺乏统一的系统评估基础设施和本体访问共享平台，导致研究碎片化，核心挑战未被充分解决。

Method: 构建OntoLearner模块化框架：统一本体访问（180个跨22领域的机器可读本体）、LLM驱动的学习流水线、标准化基准测试（含三项核心任务：术语类型标注、分类体系发现、非分类关系抽取）。基于此基础设施，评估了22个检索模型和12个LLM。

Result: 失败模式的规模与本体的复杂性相关，而非模型大小或架构复杂度。主要瓶颈是模型知识编码与本体组织结构之间的结构性错配。

Conclusion: 有效的本体学习可通过OntoLearner实现的跨领域、多任务基准测试达成；核心挑战在于知识编码与本体组织的结构性对齐，而非模型能力不足。

Abstract: Ontology learning (OL) aims to automatically construct structured knowledge models from text, yet progress remains fragmented across methods, domains, and evaluation practices. Despite decades of research, OL lacks a shared infrastructure for systematic evaluation and ontology access. This absence has hindered progress and fragmented research, leaving the central challenges of OL largely unaddressed. We introduce OntoLearner, a modular, cross-domain, and first-of-its-kind framework that unifies ontology access, large language model (LLM)-driven learning pipelines, and standardized benchmarking. OntoLearner releases 180 machine-readable ontologies spanning 22 domains and provides pipeline-ready datasets with train/dev/test splits for three core OL tasks: term typing, taxonomy discovery, and non-taxonomic relation extraction. Using this infrastructure, we conduct a large-scale empirical study of OL, evaluating 22 retrieval models and 12 LLMs across domains and tasks. The results converge on a finding that reframes the central challenge of OL: failure modes scale with ontological complexity rather than model size or architectural sophistication. The primary bottleneck is not model capability, but a structural mismatch between how models encode knowledge and how ontologies organize it. These findings establish that effective OL is reachable through the cross-domain, multi-task benchmarking enabled by OntoLearner. OntoLearner is open-source (MIT license) at https://github.com/sciknoworg/OntoLearner/.

</details>


### [182] [Multimodal Knowledge Edit-Scoped Generalization for Online Recursive MLLM Editing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01978)
*Siyuan Li,Youyuan Zhang,Ruitong Liu,Junxi Wang,Jing Li*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出ScopeEdit，一种范围感知的在线多模态大模型编辑方法，通过解耦为模态局部吸收分支和证据门控共享泛化分支，在正交低秩空间中实现范围分离的写入，有效控制了编辑的语义传播边界，在跨模态迁移与无关行为保持之间取得更好平衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有在线多模态知识编辑方法主要关注编辑可靠性和长期稳定性，但缺乏对每个编辑语义边界的控制。研究发现实例级编辑成功并不能保证有效跨模态变体的迁移，也无法防止向无关输入的泄漏，存在"范围鸿沟"问题。

Method: 提出ScopeEdit方法：1）将每次更新分解为模态局部吸收分支和证据门控共享泛化分支；2）局部分支支持稳定编辑吸收，共享分支仅在视觉和文本证据充分对齐时才允许跨模态传播；3）两分支在正交低秩空间中执行范围分离的写入几何，通过Sherman-Morrison递归维护分支级预 conditioner，实现恒定每次编辑开销。

Result: 在多种基准、长时程编辑流、不同MLLM骨干网络、真实世界VLKEB场景以及复杂视觉-语言架构上，ScopeEdit一致性地改善了范围内跨模态迁移与范围外局部性之间的权衡，同时保持了编辑可靠性、稳定性和在线效率。

Conclusion: ScopeEdit通过显式控制编辑的语义传播边界，有效解决了多模态大模型在线编辑中的"范围鸿沟"问题，为在线多模态知识编辑提供了更精细化的控制方案。

Abstract: Online multimodal knowledge editing requires injecting a continual stream of visual-textual corrections into multimodal large language models (MLLMs) with bounded overhead and minimal disruption to unrelated behaviors. Existing editors mainly emphasize edit reliability and long-horizon stability, but rarely control the semantic boundary of each edit. Our pilot analyses of post-edit behaviors and internal neuronal activities reveal a scope gap behind reliable edits: instance-level success neither guarantees transfer to valid cross-modal variants nor prevents leakage to unrelated inputs, while edit-related cross-modal responses concentrate in deeper semantic layers. Therefore, we formulate Edit-Scoped Generalization, reframing online MLLM editing from merely correcting an instance to controlling the propagation boundary of each edit. To this end, we propose ScopeEdit, a scope-aware online editor that decomposes each update into a modality-local absorption branch and an evidence-gated shared generalization branch. The local branch supports stable edit absorption, whereas the shared branch enables cross-modal propagation only when visual and textual evidence are sufficiently aligned. Both branches perform scope-separated write geometries in orthogonal low-rank spaces and maintain branch-wise preconditioners via Sherman--Morrison recursions, yielding constant per-edit overhead. Extensive experiments across diverse benchmarks, long-horizon edit streams, MLLM backbones, real-world VLKEB scenarios, and complex vision-language architectures show that ScopeEdit consistently improves the trade-off between in-scope cross-modal transfer and out-of-scope locality, while preserving edit reliability, stability and online efficiency. Our code is available at https://github.com/lab-klc/ScopeEdit.

</details>


### [183] [Traceable Fault Diagnosis for Battery Energy Storage Systems via Retrieval-Augmented Multi-Agent O&M Assistant](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01992)
*Jiangdi Ru,Bing Li,Yage Huang,Ding Wang,Keru Hua*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出了一种基于检索增强多智能体推理的可追溯BESS故障诊断助手，通过任务路由、模式约束数据库访问、多模态检索和证据综合，连接运维数据、领域知识、视觉证据和报告生成，提升诊断可靠性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大规模BESS的运维决策涉及多源异构数据（报警、电芯测量、拓扑、诊断表、历史案例、维护文档），现有监控平台仅能标记阈值越限，无法解释电压不一致、电阻漂移、短路风险、容量发散或热异常是否需要干预，缺乏可解释的诊断能力。

Method: 采用检索增强多智能体推理架构，包含BESS特定任务路由、模式约束的自然语言数据库访问、混合文本-图像检索和基于证据的答案综合。

Result: 初步内部评估在任务路由、数据库访问和诊断推理三个模块上报告了性能结果。

Conclusion: 该可追溯诊断助手能够有效整合运维数据、领域知识、视觉证据和报告生成，为BESS故障诊断提供可解释的推理支持。

Abstract: Large-scale battery energy storage systems (BESSs) require O&M decisions that combine alarms, cell-level measurements, device topology, diagnostic tables, historical cases, and maintenance documents. Monitoring platforms can flag threshold violations, but they often cannot explain whether voltage inconsistency, resistance drift, short-circuit risk, capacity divergence, or thermal abnormality needs intervention. This digest presents a traceable BESS fault-diagnosis assistant that uses retrieval-augmented multi-agent reasoning to connect operational data, domain knowledge, visual evidence, and report generation. Reliability is improved through BESS-specific task routing, schema-constrained natural-language database access, hybrid text-image retrieval, and evidence-based answer synthesis. Preliminary internal evaluation is reported for routing, database access, and diagnostic reasoning.

</details>


### [184] [InduceKV: Fixed-Footprint Continual Adaptation of Multimodal LLMs via Inducing KV Memories](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02010)
*Qianyu Chen,Ziteng Feng,Canran Xiao,Runxuan Tang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出InduceKV方法，通过将选定训练前缀存储为即插即用的注意力记忆条目（包含冻结检索键和紧凑的层间KV负载），在固定内存预算下实现多模态大语言模型的持续自适应，无需更新主干模型。该方法通过双层选择构建紧致归纳集，在任务增量指令微调、持续VQA、领域增量自适应和终身多模态指令微调等多个场景中均优于PEFT、MoE、回放和提示检索等基线方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多模态大语言模型需要在固定部署内存预算下持续适应新任务和领域，但现有方法（重复参数更新或不断增长的记忆库）会导致自适应状态随时间积累，难以满足有限部署足迹的约束。

Method: 提出InduceKV方法，将每个选定的训练前缀存储为包含冻结检索键和紧凑层间KV负载的注意力记忆条目。通过双层选择构建紧致归纳集：拟合轻量校准用于检索，同时所选记忆平衡当前任务似然、基于锚点的保留和在冻结检索空间中的覆盖度。

Result: 在任务增量指令微调、持续VQA、领域增量自适应和终身多模态指令微调等多个场景中，InduceKV在匹配内存预算条件下持续优于PEFT、MoE、回放和提示检索等基线方法。还提供了主干匹配、CoIN第一阶段、计算匹配和可扩展性诊断分析。

Conclusion: InduceKV在固定内存预算下实现了有效的持续自适应，增益并非来自更强的主干、单纯回放或无限候选池，验证了方法的有效性和鲁棒性。

Abstract: Multimodal large language models must adapt to evolving tasks and domains, yet continual improvement under bounded deployment footprint remains difficult because repeated parameter updates or growing replay stores can accumulate adaptation state over time. We study fixed-footprint continual adaptation: the deployed adaptation state is kept under a fixed memory budget, while the backbone model is left unchanged and task-specific updates are externalized. We propose InduceKV, a retrieval-based method that stores each selected training prefix as an attention-ready memory entry, consisting of a frozen retrieval key and compact layerwise key--value (KV) payloads that can be appended to the model's self-attention cache. Under a strict memory budget, InduceKV constructs a compact inducing set through bilevel selection: a lightweight calibration is fit for retrieval, while the selected memory balances current-task likelihood, anchor-based retention, and coverage in the frozen retrieval space. Across task-incremental instruction tuning, continual VQA, domain-incremental adaptation, and lifelong multimodal instruction tuning, InduceKV consistently improves over PEFT, MoE, replay, and prompt-retrieval baselines under matched memory budgets. We further report backbone-matched, stage-1 CoIN, compute-matched, and scalability diagnostics, showing that the gains are not due to a stronger backbone, replay alone, or an unbounded candidate pool.

</details>


### [185] [Hidden Forgetting in Continual Multimodal Learning: When Accuracy Survives but Grounding Fails](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02020)
*Qianyu Chen,Canran Xiao,Runxuan Tang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出RCL框架，通过冻结前序模型作为行为参考、利用反事实通道干预估计证据依赖分布，在无回放条件下同时保留任务的答案与多模态证据路径，解决LLM持续学习中答案正确但证据使用隐式遗忘的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有持续学习指标仅衡量答案正确性，忽略了多模态大模型在持续适应过程中证据使用方式（如视觉、文本、OCR、图表、文档）的稳定性，存在"隐式证据使用遗忘"这一未被关注的失效模式。

Method: 提出RCL框架：1) 冻结前一阶段模型作为行为参考；2) 通过反事实通道干预估计教师和学生模型的证据依赖分布；3) 联合优化任务学习、预测保持和依赖保持三个目标，无需回放数据且不增加推理开销。

Result: 在CoIN、COAST、MCITlib及证据敏感的多模态流上，RCL在最终性能、遗忘率、模态依赖漂移、主导证据翻转率和隐式遗忘率方面均优于无回放、PEFT、路由及记忆辅助基线。

Conclusion: 鲁棒的持续多模态学习不仅需要保留正确答案，更需要保留正确答案背后的证据路径。

Abstract: Multimodal large language models must continually adapt to evolving tasks and domains, yet standard continual learning metrics mainly measure whether old answers remain correct, leaving the stability of multimodal grounding largely unexamined. We study this overlooked failure mode and ask whether a continually adapted MLLM can preserve not only what it answers, but also how it uses visual, textual, OCR, chart, and document evidence. We identify \emph{hidden evidence-use forgetting}, where answer accuracy is retained while the model silently shifts toward different or less grounded evidence channels, and propose \textsc{RCL}, a replay-free reliance-constrained continual learning framework. \textsc{RCL} freezes the previous checkpoint as a behavioral reference, estimates teacher and student evidence-reliance profiles through counterfactual channel interventions, and jointly optimizes task learning, prediction preservation, and reliance preservation without adding inference-time cost. Across CoIN, COAST, MCITlib, and an evidence-sensitive multimodal stream, \textsc{RCL} consistently improves final performance and reduces forgetting over replay-free, PEFT, routing, and memory-assisted baselines, while substantially lowering modality reliance drift, dominant evidence flips, and hidden forgetting rates. These results suggest that robust continual multimodal learning requires preserving the evidence path behind correct answers, not merely the answers themselves.

</details>


### [186] [PACE: A Proxy for Agentic Capability Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02032)
*Yueqi Song,Lintang Sutawika,Jiarui Liu,Lindia Tjuatja,Jiayi Geng,Yunze Xiao,Daniel Lee,Aditya Bharat Soni,Vincent Lo,Xiang Yue,Graham Neubig*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出PACE框架，通过从非智能体基准测试中挑选少量原子评估实例构建代理基准，以极低成本（不足完整评估的1%）高精度（MAE<4%，Spearman>0.80）预测昂贵智能体基准测试的性能，并在14个模型、4个智能体基准和19个非智能体基准上验证了有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 评估LLM智能体在SWE-Bench、GAIA等基准上成本高昂、耗时且需要复杂基础设施，单次评估可能花费数千美元和数天时间，而测试单一能力的非智能体基准则快速廉价。因此，研究能否通过少量精心挑选的原子评估实例准确预测昂贵的智能体基准性能。

Method: 提出PACE框架，从现有非智能体评估中选取实例构建代理基准。通过结合两种互补策略（目标相关局部选择与全局信息全局选择）筛选紧凑的源实例子集，并拟合回归模型将模型在该子集上的得分映射到目标智能体基准得分。

Result: 在14个模型、4个智能体基准、19个非智能体基准上的实验表明：PACE-Bench的LOOCV MAE低于4%，Spearman相关系数高于0.80，成对模型排序准确率约85%，而成本不足完整智能体评估的1%。

Conclusion: PACE使 practitioners 能够在模型开发、选择和路由过程中可靠估计智能体性能，无需进行完整的智能体评估开销，同时通过分析所选代理实例揭示了各智能体基准独特要求的技能。

Abstract: Evaluating LLM agents on benchmarks like SWE-Bench and GAIA can be expensive, time-consuming, and requires complex infrastructure. A single evaluation can cost thousands of dollars and take days to complete. In contrast, non-agentic LLM benchmarks that test individual capabilities (e.g., reasoning, code generation) are fast and cheap to run. In this paper, we investigate whether performance on expensive agentic benchmarks can be accurately predicted by the performance on a small, carefully selected subset of atomic evaluation instances. We introduce PACE, a framework that constructs proxy benchmarks by selecting instances from existing non-agentic evaluations whose aggregate scores most reliably predict model performances on agentic benchmarks. Given a pool of candidate instances spanning atomic capabilities, PACE fits a regression that maps a model's scores on a compact subset of source instances to its score on the target agentic benchmark. The subset itself is curated by combining two complementary instance-selection strategies, target-relevance local selection and globally informative global selection. We apply PACE to the 4 target agentic benchmarks in this paper, which yields PACE-Bench, the concrete proxy benchmark that we evaluate in the paper. Experiments across 14 models, 4 agentic benchmarks, and 19 non-agentic benchmarks show that PACE-Bench predicts agentic scores with leave-one-out cross-validation (LOOCV) mean absolute error (MAE) under 4%, Spearman correlation above 0.80, and pairwise model-ranking accuracy around 85%, all at much less than 1% of the full agentic evaluation cost. We further analyze the selected proxy instances, revealing which skills each agentic benchmark uniquely demands. PACE enables practitioners to obtain reliable estimates of agentic performance during model development, selection, and routing, without the overhead of full agent evaluation.

</details>


### [187] [Algebraic Model Counting for Global Analysis of Optimal Decision Trees](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02069)
*Hiroki Arimura*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出代数决策树计数（ADTC）框架，将最优与近最优决策树的穷举分析统一为半环上的和积计算，通过动态规划在 $O^*(n^{O(Δ)})$ 时间内完成，并引入模型行为张量处理多约束，支持基于证据的模型选择。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 可解释AI中确保模型可靠性需要对假设空间进行全局评估，现有方法缺乏对最优及近最优决策树的穷举分析能力。

Method: 借鉴知识表示中的代数模型计数（AMC），将优化、计数、采样等任务统一为半环 $R$ 上的和积计算；设计 $O^*(n^{O(Δ)})$ 时间复杂度的动态规划算法；引入模型行为张量，通过张量半环上的卷积聚合多个树度量，构建模型画像。

Result: 在真实数据集上通过emtrees软件展示了ADTC在敏感领域促进基于证据的模型选择的能力。

Conclusion: ADTC为决策树的假设空间穷举分析提供了统一代数框架，有效支持多准则权衡下的可信模型选择。

Abstract: Ensuring model reliability in Explainable AI requires a global assessment of the hypothesis space. We propose a formal framework for the exhaustive analysis of optimal and near-optimal decision trees, called Algebraic Decision Tree Counting (ADTC). Inspired by Algebraic Model Counting (AMC) in knowledge representation, ADTC reformulates diverse analytical tasks, such as optimization, counting, and sampling, into a unified sum-of-products computation over a semiring $R$. While the hypothesis space of decision trees is doubly exponential with respect to the maximum depth $Δ$, our dynamic programming algorithm achieves $O^*(n^{O(Δ)})$ time complexity in the number of features $n$, where $O^*$ suppresses polynomial factors. To handle complex constraints consisting of multiple tree metrics, we introduce model behavior tensors that aggregate semiring values via convolution products over a tensor semiring. This algebraic approach efficiently constructs a model profile that captures the global landscape and trade-offs between criteria such as accuracy, size, and fairness. We demonstrate the utility of our software, emtrees, on real-world datasets, illustrating how ADTC facilitates evidence-based model selection in sensitive domains.

</details>


### [188] [Evidence-State Rewards for Long-Context Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02073)
*Ya Gao,Pekka Marttinen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: Maven是一个基于可编辑证据记忆的强化学习框架，通过定义答案条件化的证据状态价值和细粒度的动作级奖励（添加、链接、删除），在长上下文推理中显著优于仅结果RL和证据识别基线，证明了优化状态化证据导航比一次性证据提取更有效。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有长上下文RL方法通常只奖励最终答案或静态证据提取，对中间动作如何改变模型的证据状态缺乏反馈信号。

Method: 提出Maven框架，包含可编辑证据记忆；定义答案条件化的证据状态价值；对添加、链接、删除三类动作分别设计基于边际增益、事后贡献、证据协同性和改进答案支持度的奖励，并将这些奖励分配到GRPO中对应的动作跨度上。

Result: 在LongBench v2、LongReason和RULER上，使用Llama和Qwen模型，Maven优于仅结果RL和证据识别基线，生成更充分的证据集并保留更少的干扰项。

Conclusion: 长上下文RL应从一次性证据提取转向优化状态化证据导航，从而获得更好的推理性能。

Abstract: Long-context reasoning requires models to locate, revise, and synthesize evidence distributed across lengthy inputs. Existing long-context RL methods usually reward final answers or static evidence extraction, offering little feedback on how intermediate actions change the model's evidence state. We propose Maven, a reinforcement learning framework with an editable evidence memory. Maven defines an answer-conditioned evidence-state value and rewards action-level state transitions: add actions are credited by marginal gain and hindsight contribution, link actions by evidence synergy, and drop actions by improved answer support after removing misleading evidence. These rewards are assigned to the corresponding action spans in GRPO. Across Llama and Qwen models on LongBench v2, LongReason, and RULER, Maven outperforms outcome-only RL and evidence-identification baselines, producing more sufficient evidence sets and lower distractor retention. Our results show that long-context RL benefits from optimizing stateful evidence navigation rather than one-shot evidence extraction.

</details>


### [189] [SUNTA: Hierarchical Video Prediction with Surprise-based Chunking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02087)
*Tomoshi Iiyama,Masahiro Suzuki,Yutaka Matsuo*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出SUNTA方法，利用预测误差（惊喜）作为分块依据，通过解耦训练策略和内部不一致性度量，在层次化状态空间模型中实现长时序预测，在2D/3D视频预测中显著优于基线（250步保持准确，基线10步内退化）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有HSSM采用固定长度或相似性分块，无法对齐数据的固有时间结构；作者认为分块应由预测误差驱动，因为预测误差更直接指示何时需要长程上下文。

Method: 提出SUNTA：(1) 采用解耦训练策略避免端到端训练中的层次坍塌；(2) 在开放环预测中使用内部不一致性作为自上而下的惊喜度量来确定想象展开中的分块边界。

Result: 在2D和3D环境的视频预测任务中，SUNTA在250时间步内保持准确预测，而所有基线方法在前10步内即退化。

Conclusion: 基于惊喜的分块策略结合解耦训练能有效解决HSSM中的层次坍塌和惊喜信号缺失问题，显著提升长时序预测性能。

Abstract: Hierarchical state-space models (HSSMs) offer a promising approach to long-horizon prediction by segmenting sequences into temporal chunks. However, their performance hinges on how chunk boundaries are determined. While prior HSSMs typically rely on fixed-length chunking or similarity-based boundary detection, these methods often misalign with the intrinsic temporal structure of the data. We argue that chunking should instead be driven by prediction errors, which more directly indicate when longer-range context becomes necessary. Nevertheless, integrating surprise-based chunking into HSSMs introduces critical challenges, including hierarchical collapse during end-to-end training and the absence of surprise signals during open-loop prediction. To address these issues, we propose Surprise-based Nested Temporal Abstraction (SUNTA), a method that employs a decoupled training strategy to preserve surprise signals and uses internal inconsistency as a top-down surprise metric to determine chunk boundaries within imagined rollouts. Experiments on video prediction tasks in 2D and 3D environments demonstrate that SUNTA outperforms baselines, uniquely maintaining accurate predictions over 250 timesteps, whereas all baselines degrade within the first 10 timesteps.

</details>


### [190] [ContextNest: Verifiable Context Governance for Autonomous AI Agent](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02116)
*Misha Sulpovar,Benn R. Konsynski,Qaish Kanchwala,Gabe Goodhart*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出ContextNext，一个为AI代理提供上下文治理的开放规范与参考实现。它在RAG检索层之下运作，确保知识工件的批准、版本、归因和完整性。实验表明，在过期版本攻击中，治理选择在答案质量和token成本上均优于BM25；在检索确定性测试中，确定性选择器与BM25完全稳定，而稠密+HNSW方法在80%查询上出现非确定性结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自主AI代理依赖外部知识库，但现有检索管道仅提供相关性，缺乏对知识工件的来源、版本身份、完整性、可追溯性和时间点重建的持久保证。作者将这一问题形式化为"上下文治理"，旨在填补检索质量之外的治理空白。

Method: ContextNext规范包括：类型化Markdown文档与元数据、确定性集合代数选择器、contextnest:// URI引用、SHA-256哈希链版本历史、图级检查点、通过模型上下文协议(MCP)连接实时数据的源节点，以及代理上下文消费的审计追踪。

Result: 实验一（过期版本攻击）：治理选择严格帕累托支配BM25稀疏检索，答案质量通过率97%对93-90%，输入token成本仅为约1/3。实验二（检索确定性）：在1,060文档语料库上，确定性选择器和BM25在重复相同查询时返回完全一致的文档集（Jaccard=1.0），而稠密+HNSW基线在80%查询上非确定（平均Jaccard=0.611，最差0.210）。

Conclusion: 上下文治理能够解决单纯检索质量无法应对的失效模式。团队以开源许可发布了核心引擎、CLI和MCP服务器。

Abstract: Autonomous AI agents increasingly depend on external knowledge stores, yet most retrieval pipelines provide relevance without durable guarantees of provenance, version identity, integrity, traceability, or point-in-time reconstruction. We formalize this as context governance and present ContextNext, an open specification and reference implementation for governed AI-consumable knowledge vaults. ContextNext does not replace Retrieval-Augmented Generation (RAG); it supplies the governance layer beneath retrieval, determining which artifacts are approved, current, attributable, and integrity-verified before retrieval systems operate over them.
  The specification combines typed Markdown documents with metadata, deterministic set-algebraic selectors, contextnest:// URI references, SHA-256 hash-chained version histories, graph-level checkpoints, source nodes for live data through the Model Context Protocol (MCP), and audit traces of agent context consumption. These mechanisms let organizations reconstruct which knowledge versions informed an agent output and whether those versions were AI-eligible when consumed.
  We report first empirical results from two controlled experiments. In a stale-version attack isolating the governance-versus-retrieval failure mode, governed selection strictly Pareto-dominates BM25 sparse retrieval, with higher answer-quality pass rate (97% versus 93-90%) at about one-third the input-token cost. In a retrieval-determinism experiment over a 1,060-document corpus, deterministic selectors and BM25 return stable document sets across repeated identical queries (Jaccard 1.0), while a dense+HNSW baseline is non-deterministic on 80% of queries (mean Jaccard 0.611, worst case 0.210). These results suggest that context governance addresses failure modes retrieval quality alone is not designed to resolve. We release a core engine, CLI, and MCP server under open licenses.

</details>


### [191] [Enhancing Fitness Intelligence through Domain-Specific LLM Post-Training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02118)
*Xingtao Zhao,Tian Yang,Han Jiang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文介绍FitOne，一系列基于Qwen3的健身领域大语言模型（8B/32B参数），通过三阶段后训练（持续预训练、监督微调、强化学习）提升科学健身教练领域的专业性和可靠性，在ACSM-EP和NSCA-CSCS专业认证考试上相比基座模型提升最高12.73%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统科学健身教练依赖人类专业人员，成本高且难以普及；通用大语言模型在健身领域缺乏领域知识整合，在复杂健身场景中表现不佳。因此亟需构建专用于健身领域、兼具通用能力与专业知识的LLM系统。

Method: 以Qwen3为基座模型，采用三阶段后训练流水线：(1) 持续预训练（大规模高质量数据集）；(2) 监督微调；(3) 强化学习。训练数据来源于严格的知识工程流程构建的大规模高质量数据集。

Result: 在ACSM-EP和NSCA-CSCS专业认证考试上，FitOne-8B/32B相比Qwen3基座模型平均提升10.09%/9.29%和12.73%/7.01%，同时保持强大的通用能力（知识推理、指令遵循）。消融实验验证了每个训练阶段的必要性。

Conclusion: FitOne成功平衡了领域专业知识增强与通用能力保持，推动了LLM系统向更可靠的健身智能发展，为未来领域专用LLM的研究提供了启发。

Abstract: Scientific Fitness Coaching (SFC) is typically delivered by human professionals, making it costly and inaccessible to many. While recent advances in Large Language Models (LLMs) show considerable promise for more inclusive fitness coaching, directly deploying prevailing general-purpose LLMs in SFC reveals critical limitations. These models often lack sufficient domain-specific knowledge integration, leading to weak performance on complex SFC scenarios. In this paper, we introduce FitOne, a series of fitness LLMs (with 8B and 32B parameters) designed to improve reliability and domain specialization for SFC applications. Built upon the Qwen3 foundation models, FitOne is developed through a three-stage post-training pipeline consisting of continual pre-training, supervised fine-tuning, and reinforcement learning, using large-scale, high-quality datasets derived from rigorous knowledge engineering. We conduct comprehensive evaluations of FitOne on professional fitness certification exams, including ACSM-EP and NSCA-CSCS, as well as general capabilities such as knowledge reasoning and instruction following. Experimental results show that, while retaining strong general capabilities, FitOne-8B/32B achieves average improvements of up to 10.09%/9.29% and 12.73%/7.01% on the ACSM-EP and NSCA-CSCS exams, respectively, compared with the Qwen3 base models. Furthermore, in-depth ablation studies confirm the necessity of each training stage, highlighting the pipeline's effectiveness in balancing domain expertise enhancement with general ability retention. We believe this research advances LLM systems toward more reliable fitness intelligence and will inspire future research on developing domain-specific LLMs.

</details>


### [192] [Coding-agents can replicate scientific machine learning papers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02134)
*Atharva Hans,Ilias Bilionis*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出 Paper-replication 工作流，将论文中的计算性声明转化为可记录、可验证的目标，并通过编码智能体自动重建方法、运行实验、链接证据与原文声明，最终通过验证检查完成复现。在四篇科学机器学习论文的十二次独立运行中，所有目标和工作区均通过完成验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 科学机器学习论文通常包含计算性声明（如误差低于5%或预测区间覆盖测试数据），但仅靠提示词引导编码智能体复现这些声明，无法可靠地保留进展或验证生成的证据是否真正支持论文的声明。

Method: Paper-replication 工作流将每条选定的论文声明设为带有记录证据的目标。智能体依次记录目标、重建论文方法、运行计算实验、将输出链接到来源并与论文声明比较、记录匹配证据在复现报告中的位置，并在完成前通过验证检查。

Result: 在四篇论文的十二次独立运行中，所有工作区均通过完成门控，158个记录目标均被报告覆盖。但重复运行在目标划分方式、数值保真度、复现耗时、中间执行替换次数以及证据接受规则等方面存在差异。

Conclusion: Paper-replication 将完成条件从依赖智能体的最终消息转变为依赖工作区证据和验证检查，从而更可靠地保证复现质量。

Abstract: Scientific machine learning papers typically make computational claims, e.g., that the relative mean square error is less than 5% or that the 95% predictive credible interval covers the test data. A coding agent can be prompted to replicate those claims from paper materials alone, but the prompt does not by itself reliably preserve progress or check whether generated evidence supports the paper's claims. We introduce Paper-replication, a workflow that makes each selected paper claim a target with recorded evidence, and implement it as a coding-agent skill. The workflow makes the agent record those targets, reconstruct the paper's method, run computational experiments, link generated outputs to provenance and comparisons with the paper's claims, record where matched evidence appears in the replication report, and pass validation checks before completion. We evaluate Paper-replication on twelve independent runs across four scientific machine learning papers. All twelve workspaces pass the completion gate, and all 158 recorded targets are matched with report coverage. Even in this completed workspace state, repeated runs differ in how papers are divided into targets, in numerical fidelity to the source papers, in elapsed replication time, in the number of intermediate executions replaced before final evidence is accepted, and in the rules used to accept evidence. Paper-replication makes completion depend on workspace evidence and validation checks rather than on the agent's final message.

</details>


### [193] [A$^{2}$utoLPBench: An Auto-Generated, Agent-Friendly LP Benchmark via Inverse-KKT Construction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02141)
*Shuo Ren,Yaohui Han,Yifan Shi,Libo Shen,Haodong Lu,Dongfang Wu,Rongliang Fu,Bei Yu,Tsung-Yi Ho*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: A²utoLPBench是一个基于生成器的线性规划文本问题基准测试，通过构造法确保答案正确性，提供无限量新问题、可调节难度、防数据泄露等特性，支持LLM驱动智能体一键运行并获得可重复评分。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LP-from-text基准多为静态手工标注数据集，存在规模固定、难度固定、题目可能泄露到LLM训练数据中等缺陷，亟需一个可动态生成、防泄露、低成本且结果可靠的评估方案。

Method: 先选取可行点和其对偶变量，然后构造一个以该点为最优解且目标值已知的线性规划问题（无需调用求解器或人工标注）；提供参考求解器-评论基线、Docker镜像和供LLM智能体读取的使用说明，支持一键运行；作为生成器而非固定数据集，可通过参数(n,m)控制难度，使用截止日期后的随机种子防止数据泄露。

Result: 生成了无限量新问题，确保了答案构造性正确，降低了每道题相对于人工编写的LLM侧成本，实现了跨独立批次的重复评分，并有效抵抗训练数据泄露。

Conclusion: A²utoLPBench通过生成器范式解决了固定数据集的根本局限，为LLM驱动智能体评估线性规划问题提供了可扩展、可靠、抗泄露的基准测试方案。

Abstract: Most LP-from-text benchmarks are static datasets of word problems written and labeled by hand. Once such a dataset is released, its size is fixed, its difficulty is fixed, and every problem can leak into the training data of future LLMs. We present \textbf{A$^{2}$utoLPBench}, a benchmark for testing LLM-driven agents on linear programming problems written in plain text. We first pick a feasible point and dual, then write down a problem for which that point is optimal and the objective value is known. The answer is known by construction, with no solver call and no human annotator. The evaluation environment bundles a reference solver-critic baseline and a Docker image whose usage instructions are written for an LLM-driven agent to read. With these in place, any agent can run the benchmark and get a calibrated score with one command. Because the benchmark is a generator rather than a fixed dataset, it has properties no fixed dataset can match: an unlimited supply of fresh problems, a difficulty knob set by $(n,m)$, ground-truth answers correct by construction, low LLM-side cost per problem relative to human authoring, repeatable scores across independent batches, and resistance to training-data leakage when fresh post-cutoff seed ranges are used.

</details>


### [194] [A rubric-based controlled comparison of frontier language models on expert-authored clinical reasoning tasks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02175)
*Samiha A. Ismail,Fan X. Chen,Ali Merali*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出一个由临床医生撰写的小型但高难度医学评估数据集（5个临床场景，184条MECE标准），评估三个前沿大模型（GPT 5.4、Claude Opus 4.7、Gemini 3.1 Pro），发现关键临床优先级反转：高权重（权重5）标准通过率仅32.4-41.7%，而低权重标准通过率达80-90%，52%的关键标准未被任何模型满足。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有选择题医学基准日益饱和，而开放式的临床评估（如HealthBench）显示成绩仍很低（Hard子集最高32%），需要更具挑战性和临床相关性的评估方法。

Method: 构建5个由临床医生撰写的临床场景（覆盖麻醉、内科/家庭医学、急诊医学、产科），每个场景附带原子化、加权、MECE评分标准（共184条），并由临床医生撰写黄金答案。评估GPT 5.4、Claude Opus 4.7、Gemini 3.1 Pro三个前沿模型，并使用三个LLM自动评分器复现专家标注。

Result: 平均通过率：Claude 0.47，GPT 0.39，Gemini 0.37。关键发现为临床优先级反转：权重5（关键）标准通过率仅32.4-41.7%，权重1标准达80-90%；108条关键标准中56条（52%）未被任何模型满足。LLM自动评分器与专家标注一致率达92.8-94.7%。

Conclusion: 本文作为方法学和初步发现贡献，展示了可扩展、可防御的评估流程，为构建大规模医学基准奠定基础。

Abstract: Multiple-choice medical benchmarks are increasingly saturated, and recent rubric-based evaluations such as HealthBench have shown that open-ended clinical performance is far from solved - its "Hard" subset top score remains 32%. We present a small, deliberately difficult evaluation dataset of five clinician-authored clinical scenarios spanning four specialties (anaesthesia, internal/family medicine, emergency medicine, and obstetrics), each accompanied by an atomic, weighted, MECE rubric (25-62 criteria per task; 184 criteria total) authored from a clinician-drafted golden answer. We evaluate three frontier models: GPT 5.4, Claude Opus 4.7, and Gemini 3.1 Pro. Mean rubric pass rates were 0.47 (Claude), 0.39 (GPT), and 0.37 (Gemini). The central finding is an inversion of clinical priority: the highest-weighted (weight-5, critical) criteria passed at only 32.4-41.7%, while low-stakes weight-1 criteria passed at 80-90%. 56 of 108 critical (weight-5) criteria (52%) were satisfied by no model. Three LLM autoraters reproduced expert met/not-met labels on 92.8-94.7% of 552 graded criteria. We position this as a methods-and-preliminary-findings contribution: the five tasks demonstrate a scalable, defensible pipeline ready to develop into a large-scale benchmark.

</details>


### [195] [UA-ChatDev: Uncertainty-Aware Multi-Agent Collaboration for Reliable Software Development](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02186)
*Temitayo Olamilekan Ogunsusi,Lijun Qian,Xishuang Dong*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出UA-ChatDev，一个不确定性感知的多智能体软件开发框架，通过集成不确定性量化（基于token级对数概率）和阶段感知阈值校准，在不确定性高时触发检索验证，从而防止错误在开发阶段间传播，在SRDD基准上优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM驱动的多智能体软件开发框架假设中间输出同样可靠，导致早期阶段的错误（幻觉）会传播到下游智能体，影响最终软件质量。

Method: 1) 基于token级对数概率的轻量级不确定性估计机制评估智能体响应置信度；2) 阶段感知阈值校准，在不确定性超过可接受水平时选择性触发基于检索的验证。

Result: 在SRDD基准上，UA-ChatDev在完整性、可执行性、一致性和整体质量指标上持续优于现有单智能体和多智能体软件开发框架。消融实验和通信分析验证了不确定性感知交互增强了代码执行可靠性。

Conclusion: 不确定性量化能有效缓解多智能体软件开发中的幻觉传播问题，提升最终软件质量。

Abstract: Software development is a complex task that demands cooperation among agents with diverse roles. Large language models (LLMs) have enabled autonomous multi-agent software development frameworks that leverage role-based collaboration to automate requirements analysis, coding, testing, and refinement. However, existing approaches typically assume that intermediate agent outputs are equally reliable, leaving them vulnerable to hallucination propagation, where incorrect decisions generated in early development phases are transferred to downstream agents and negatively impact final software quality. To address this challenge, we propose UA-ChatDev, an uncertainty-aware multi-agent software development framework that integrates uncertainty quantification into agent interactions. It introduces a lightweight uncertainty estimation mechanism based on token-level log probabilities to assess the confidence of agent responses and employs phase-aware threshold calibration to selectively trigger retrieval-based verification when uncertainty exceeds acceptable levels. Extensive experiments on the SRDD benchmark demonstrate that UA-ChatDev consistently outperforms existing single-agent and multi-agent software development frameworks across completeness, executability, consistency, and overall quality metrics. Further ablation studies and communication analyses verify that uncertainty-aware interactions enhance code execution reliability.

</details>


### [196] [Criticality-Based Guard Rail Validation for AI Agent Decisions in Autonomous Telecom Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02210)
*Ravi Kant Sharma*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出Guard Rail Validation (GRV)框架，一种标准化的运行时架构，用于在AI/ML决策触发网络状态变更前进行拦截和验证，支持电信网络向完全自治（Level 4-5）演进。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前缺乏标准化的运行时机制来拦截和验证AI/ML推理输出，导致自治网络中错误决策可能直接触发网络状态变更，存在安全隐患。

Method: GRV框架：按动作范围/类型、服务关键性、自治等级、可逆性、时间行为模式等多加权维度评估决策关键性，并据此应用分级验证机制（执行并记录、边界检查、独立代理验证、多代理共识），同时提供跨代理冲突检测和运行时合规日志。

Result: 给出了架构、算法流程、O-RAN部署模型，并评估了对电信已知AI/ML攻击的威胁覆盖范围。

Conclusion: GRV框架为自治网络提供了一种可标准化的运行时决策验证方案，有助于满足EU AI Act第14条等监管合规要求。

Abstract: The evolution toward fully autonomous telecommunications networks (Autonomous Network Levels 4-5) requires AI/ML agents to make real-time network decisions without human intervention. However, no standardized runtime mechanism exists to intercept and validate individual inference outputs before they trigger live network state changes, creating risks of erroneous autonomous decisions. This paper proposes the Guard Rail Validation (GRV) framework, a standardizable runtime architecture for intercepting and validating AI-driven decisions before execution. The framework evaluates decisions across multiple weighted dimensions -- including action scope, action type, service criticality, agent autonomy level, reversibility, and temporal behavioural patterns -- to determine a criticality level. Based on this level, graduated validation mechanisms are applied: execute-with-logging, bounds checking, independent agent validation, or multi-agent consensus. The framework additionally provides cross-agent conflict detection with criticality-weighted priority resolution and runtime conformance logging for regulatory compliance (e.g., EU AI Act Article 14). We present the architecture, algorithmic procedures, O-RAN deployment model, and evaluate threat coverage against known AI/ML attacks in telecommunications.

</details>


### [197] [Purified OPSD: On-Policy Self-Distillation Without Losing How to Think](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02234)
*Zhanming Shen,Jintao Tong,Shaotian Yan,Chen Shen,Hao Chen,Wentao Ye,Xiaomeng Hu,Rui Miao,Haobo Wang,Junbo Zhao,Gang Chen,Jieping Ye*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文发现on-policy self-distillation (OPSD)在长思维链推理模型中失效，原因是教师监督信号被参考诱导的捷径学习主导，而问题条件化的可迁移成分被抑制。作者提出通过参考-only教师和PMI机制提取可迁移的监督信号，在四个长CoT模型上取得一致改进。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: OPSD在长思维链推理模型中失效，需要诊断原因并提出解决方案。

Method: 首先对教师监督信号进行分解，识别出参考诱导成分和问题条件化成分；然后构建参考-only教师（仅以参考为条件，不包含问题）来分离不可迁移成分；最后利用PMI将残差信号转化为适合学生蒸馏的目标分布，过滤掉参考诱导的捷径。

Result: 在四个长CoT模型和两个数据集上，该方法一致优于基线和标准OPSD，且能保持模型自然的认知行为。

Conclusion: OPSD的失效源于监督信号被参考诱导成分主导，所提方法通过分解和PMI机制有效过滤了非迁移信号，实现了对长CoT推理模型的有效蒸馏。

Abstract: On-policy self-distillation (OPSD) has emerged as a promising paradigm for improving LLM reasoning, where a privileged teacher with access to reference solutions provides token-level supervision on the student's own generated trajectories. However, we find that OPSD consistently fails on long chain-of-thought (long-CoT) reasoning models, yielding at best marginal gains while destabilizing the reflective reasoning capability these models depend on. Through a novel decomposition of the teacher's supervision signal, we identify the root cause: the teacher's supervision is dominated by a reference-induced component that drives rote memorization of reference-specific shortcuts, while the question-conditioned, inference-transferable component is ignored or actively opposed. Based on this diagnosis, we propose a two-step solution. First, we construct a reference-only teacher (the same model conditioned on the reference without the question) to isolate the non-transferable component of the supervision signal; the residual after subtracting this component captures the question-conditioned, inference-transferable correction. Second, we use pointwise mutual information (PMI) as the mechanism to transform this residual into a well-formed PMI target distribution that the student can directly distill from, filtering out the reference-induced shortcut. Experiments on four long-CoT models across two datasets demonstrate consistent improvements over both the base model and standard OPSD, while preserving the models' natural epistemic behavior throughout training.

</details>


### [198] [Copewell: A Multi-Agent Swarm Architecture for Equitable Mental Wellness Support](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02245)
*Seren Yenikent,Jack Vinijtrongjit,Katherine Ng*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: Copewell是一个多智能体群体系统，通过整合多源评估、效价-唤醒情绪映射和双模式干预，旨在扩大心理健康的可及性，并内置隐私保护与伦理监督机制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 全球近10亿人受心理健康障碍困扰，但中低收入国家75%的患者因劳动力短缺、成本障碍和污名化得不到治疗。现有AI健康解决方案主要依赖单一模式的对话界面，存在高弃用率且无法根据用户动态情绪状态提供可测量的即时缓解。

Method: 提出三项技术创新：(1) 多源评估框架，整合自我报告、生理和情境数据以减少算法偏差；(2) 基于Russell情感环形模型的效价-唤醒情绪映射，将用户路由至专门的AI智能体；(3) 双模式干预，结合对话支持与基于证据的感觉健康方案。此外设计了隐私优先架构、专门的伦理监督智能体以及由心理健康从业者参与的共同设计流程。

Result: 早期从业者参与和beta部署为设计决策提供了信息，并确定了未来实证评估的方向。

Conclusion: 通过展示技术架构如何从初始阶段将公平与安全原则可操作化，为负责任AI话语做出贡献。

Abstract: Mental health disorders affect nearly one billion people globally, yet 75% of individuals in low- and middle-income countries receive no treatment due to workforce shortages, cost barriers, and stigma. Current AI-powered wellness solutions predominantly rely on single-mode conversational interfaces that suffer high abandonment rates and fail to provide measurable, immediate relief calibrated to users' dynamic emotional states. This paper presents Copewell, a novel multi-agent swarm system designed to expand access to mental wellness support through human-centered AI principles. Our architecture introduces three technical innovations: (1) a multi-source assessment framework integrating self-reported, physiological, and contextual data to mitigate algorithmic bias; (2) valence-arousal emotion mapping using Russell's Circumplex Model of Affect to route users to specialized AI agents; and (3) dual-mode intervention delivery combining conversational support with evidence-based sensory wellness protocols. We examine the sociotechnical design considerations underlying Copewell's development, including a privacy-first architecture, embedded ethical oversight through a dedicated Ethics Supervisor agent, and participatory design informed by mental health practitioners. Early practitioner engagement and beta deployment inform design decisions and identify directions for future empirical evaluation. This work contributes to responsible AI discourse by demonstrating how technical architecture can operationalize equity and safety principles from inception.

</details>


### [199] [AgenticSTS: A Bounded-Memory Testbed for Long-Horizon LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02255)
*Xiangchen Cheng,Yunwei Jiang,Jianwen Sun,Zizhen Li,Chuanhao Li,Xiangcheng Cao,Yihao Liu,Fanrui Zhang,Li Jin,Kaipeng Zhang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出一种"有界契约"记忆机制：LLM智能体的每个决策仅通过类型化检索从新鲜用户消息中构建，而非拼接原始跨决策记录。在《杀戮尖塔2》游戏中验证，加入战略技能层可将胜率从3/10提升至6/10（虽未达统计显著性），并开源了包含298条轨迹的可复现测试平台。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统长程LLM智能体将历史观测、工具调用和反思全部拼接到每个提示中，导致上下文混杂、单个记忆组件的影响难以隔离。亟需一种有边界的、可隔离分析各记忆层效果的记忆契约。

Method: 提出"有界契约"：每个决策由类型化检索（typed retrieval）组装的新鲜用户消息驱动，不拼接原始跨决策对话记录，保证提示长度有界且每一层可单独消融。在《杀戮尖塔2》封闭规则随机卡牌游戏中实例化该契约。

Result: 固定A0消融实验中，无存储基线胜率3/10，加入战略技能层后6/10（Fisher精确检验p≈0.37，方向性而非统计决定性）；跨骨干网络探测和公开累积上下文基线作为操作对比报告。

Conclusion: 开源了可复现测试平台（298条轨迹、冻结记忆/技能快照、提示记录和分析脚本），为研究显式记忆层如何塑造长程LLM智能体决策提供了经过验证的可重用方法论。

Abstract: Memory for a long-horizon LLM agent is a contract about what each future decision is allowed to see. The simplest contract appends past observations, tool calls, and reflections to every prompt, which makes prior context easy to access but also turns it into a jumbled mixture in which the effect of any single memory component is hard to isolate. We introduce and instrument an alternative bounded contract: every decision is made from a fresh user message assembled by typed retrieval, with no raw cross-decision transcript appended. The prompt thus stays bounded across runs of any length, and any single layer can be ablated in isolation. We instantiate the contract in Slay the Spire 2, a closed-rule stochastic deck-building game whose runs require hundreds of tactical and strategic decisions. A public online benchmark of frontier LLMs on the same game reports zero wins at the lowest difficulty across five configurations, and the developer-reported human win rate at the same difficulty is 16%; the task is hard but not saturated. Within our harness, a fixed-A0 ablation shows the largest observed difference when triggered strategic skills are enabled: the no-store baseline wins 3/10 games and adding the skill layer 6/10. At this sample size the comparison is directional rather than statistically decisive (Fisher exact p\approx0.37); a cross-backbone probe and public accumulating-context baselines are reported as operational comparisons rather than controlled tests of the contract variable itself. We release a reproducible testbed: 298 completed trajectories with condition tags, frozen memory/skill snapshots, prompt records, and analysis scripts -- an agent design and a validated, reusable methodology for studying how explicit memory layers shape long-horizon LLM-agent decisions.

</details>


### [200] [A Hippocampus for Linear Attention: An Exact Memory for What the Recurrent State Forgets](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02303)
*Wanyun Cui*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: HOLA模型通过为线性注意力添加海马体互补的有限精确KV缓存，在保持O(1)内存的同时显著提升了长序列中关键信息的召回能力，340M参数训练15B token后困惑度降低16.1%，并支持32k长度的外推。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 线性注意力和状态空间模型将前缀压缩为固定大小的循环状态，虽然实现了O(1)内存复杂度，但存在有损精确记忆的问题：当大量键值关联竞争时，早期事实被覆盖，导致"针在海草堆中"的召回性能下降。

Method: 借鉴互补学习系统理论，HOLA保留原有的delta-rule状态作为压缩记忆，同时增加一个有界的精确KV缓存，形成半参数化的测试时记忆。缓存写入无需学习式驱逐模块，而是保留那些具有较大beta*||e||（即实际提交到状态的预测残差）的token；通过解耦的RMSNorm-gamma缓存读取机制，将精确KV对转化为锐利检索而非软平均。

Result: 340M参数在15B SlimPajama tokens上训练后，HOLA将Wikitext困惑度从27.32降至22.92（-16.1%），低于全注意力Transformer++（26.88）；LAMBADA困惑度从30.95降至30.26。在线性上下文检索中达到最优，在RULER针在海草堆任务中保持32k token（16倍训练长度）的鲁棒性，远优于GDN或匹配的HOLA+近期缓存。

Conclusion: 通过海马体互补的精确缓存机制，线性注意力模型可以在保持高效内存的同时显著提升长距离精确记忆能力，实现比全注意力模型更优的困惑度表现和更强的长序列外推能力。

Abstract: Linear-attention and state-space language models compress the prefix into a fixed-size recurrent state, yielding O(1) memory at the cost of a lossy exact memory: when many key--value associations compete, earlier facts are overwritten and needle recall degrades. Inspired by Complementary Learning Systems, we give linear attention a hippocampal complement. HOLA (Hippocampal Linear Attention) keeps the usual delta-rule state as a compressive memory and adds a bounded exact KV cache, forming a semiparametric test-time memory: the state models linearly compressible structure, while the cache stores associations that should not be forced through that state. The cache writes without a learned eviction module, keeping tokens with large beta * ||e||, the prediction residual actually committed to the state; a decoupled RMSNorm-gamma cache read then turns these exact KV pairs into sharp retrieval rather than soft averaging. At 340M parameters trained on 15B SlimPajama tokens, HOLA lowers Wikitext perplexity from 27.32 to 22.92 (-16.1%), below a full-attention Transformer++ (26.88), and improves LAMBADA perplexity from 30.95 to 30.26. It also achieves the best linear in-context retrieval and remains much more robust than GDN or a matched HOLA+recency cache on RULER needle-in-a-haystack recall out to 32k tokens (16x its training length).

</details>


### [201] [Grounded autonomous research: a fault-tolerant LLM pipeline from corpus to manuscript in frontier computational physics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02329)
*Haonan Huang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出了一种从arXiv论文语料库到发表级科学手稿的全自动端到端流水线，通过文献锚定和外部分布式验证替代传统的沙箱执行校准，在凝聚态物理领域（交替磁体压电磁性）自主完成了从研究方向构思到第一性原理计算再到论文撰写的全过程，实现了47个会话、2162次文献咨询、仅需有限人工干预的自主研究。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自主研究智能体在机器学习沙箱中效果良好，但在前沿物理科学中存在根本性不同：物理推理贯穿方法选择、工具链文档不足、校准必须依赖外部文献锚点而非沙箱执行——无框架支持的智能体只能引用但无法直面文献，会从内部先验中幻觉出看似合理但不可验证的结果。因此需要一种结构化的文献锚定和数值校准机制。

Method: 构建了一个六阶段流水线，包含47个全新上下文（fresh-context）会话，仅通过磁盘共享状态；采用三个核心设计原则：（1）冗余容错——全新上下文隔离、分布式接地（distributed grounding）和对抗性审查捕捉单会话遗漏；（2）分阶段自治——预试和试后阶段完全自主，试阶段仅在复现失败时需有限人工干预（操作知识整理，非科学方向指导）；（3）结构化数值对抗——通过校准检查点的数值面对接强制文献锚定，并设置了预架构基线和无试消融两个成对失败模式来隔离该机制的作用。

Result: 从11,083篇近期凝聚态物理arXiv论文出发，自主构思研究方向、映射语料库、通过复现已发表参考文献校准方法论、执行原创第一性原理计算，最终产出一篇包含三个实质性物理发现（关于交替磁体压电磁性）的发表级手稿，全程文献引导，共2162次文献咨询。两个成对失败模式实验证明，校准检查点的结构化数值面对是实现文献接地（grounding）的关键操作机制。

Conclusion: 该方法在计算物理之外的、高风险科学领域（如需要严格文献验证和物理推理的领域）为自主研究奠定了基础。其核心贡献在于：明确了自主研究的原语（primitives）、刻画了失败模式、量化了干预模式——这些要素可推广至更广泛的科学自主发现场景。

Abstract: Autonomous-research agents have demonstrated end-to-end LLM automation in machine-learning sandboxes where execution provides calibration. Frontier physical science differs categorically: physical reasoning underlies every methodology choice, toolchains are often underdocumented, and calibration must come from external literature anchors - which unscaffolded agents cite but do not confront, hallucinating plausible, unverifiable results from internal priors. We present a pipeline that runs end-to-end from a corpus of 11,083 recent condensed-matter physics arXiv papers to a publication-grade manuscript with three substantive physics findings (here on altermagnetic piezomagnetism): the agent autonomously conceives a research direction by mapping the corpus, calibrates methodology by reproducing published references, conducts novel first-principles computations, and writes the manuscript - grounded in literature throughout, across 47 fresh-context sessions in six phases sharing only on-disk state, with 2,162 literature-consultation events. Fault tolerance emerges from redundancy: fresh-context isolation, distributed grounding, and adversarial review catch what any single session misses; pre- and post-pilot stages are fully autonomous, and pilot requires bounded human intervention only at reproduction failures - operational knowledge curation, not scientific direction. Two paired failure modes - a pre-architecture baseline and a no-pilot ablation - isolate structurally enforced numerical confrontation at calibration checkpoints as the operative grounding mechanism. The primitives, characterized failure modes, and quantified intervention pattern lay a foundation for autonomous research in high-stakes scientific domains beyond computational physics.

</details>


### [202] [DRIFTLENS: Measuring Memory-Induced Reasoning Drift in Personalized Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02374)
*Xi Fang,Weijie Xu,Yingqiang Ge,Yuhui Xu,Stephanie Eckman,Chandan K. Reddy*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文提出DRIFTLENS框架，发现用户属性记忆会导致LLM推理轨迹发生显著漂移，即使最终答案仍然流畅合理，现有后训练方法（GRPO/DPO）只能部分缓解这一问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 个性化记忆会改变模型对用户的回复内容，但现有研究尚未系统评估这种记忆是否以及在多大程度上改变了模型用于支撑其回答的推理轨迹（reasoning trajectory），尤其是在没有单一标准答案的开放式问题上。

Method: 提出DRIFTLENS框架，将每个推理步骤映射到价值类别，测量无记忆轨迹与注入用户属性记忆的轨迹之间的分歧。在4个LLM和10种用户属性上评估，并使用GRPO/DPO后训练方法尝试减少漂移。

Result: 用户属性记忆会导致中等至大规模的推理漂移（超过模型的语用噪声基线），即使最终答案流畅、相关且合理。GRPO和DPO均可减少漂移，但无统一最优方案，对下游能力、有用性和指令遵循的影响因模型和奖励函数而异。

Conclusion: 记忆诱导的推理漂移是一个可测量且仅能部分缓解的失败模式，提醒个性化LLM需要关注推理过程而非仅最终答案的质量。

Abstract: Personalization changes what a model says to a user; we show that it can also change the reasoning trajectory used to justify the response. Modern LLMs personalize interactions by storing user attributes, preferences, and prior context, then injecting this information into future prompts. We study whether such memory reshapes reasoning on open-ended questions where no single ground-truth answer exists. To quantify this effect, we introduce DRIFTLENS, a ground-truth-free framework that maps each expressed reasoning step to a value category and measures divergence between a question's no-memory trajectory and its trajectory under injected user-attribute memory. We first validate that DRIFTLENS distinguishes content-free pragmatic noise from substantive reasoning changes. Across four LLMs and 10 user-attribute categories, including age, occupation, and disability, user-attribute memory induces medium-to-large reasoning drift above each model's pragmatic-noise floor, even when final answers remain fluent, on-topic, and plausible. We then evaluate GRPO- and DPO-based post-training methods for reducing drift. Both reduce drift, but neither uniformly dominates; effects on downstream capability, helpfulness, and instruction following are model-and reward-dependent. These results suggest that memory-induced reasoning drift is a measurable and only partly mitigated failure mode of personalized language models.

</details>


### [203] [Hardware-Enforced Semantic Coordination for Safety-Critical Real-Time Autonomous Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02376)
*Uwe M. Borghoff,Paolo Bottoni,Remo Pareschi*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 针对自主AI系统中软件协调在实时部署中的局限性（延迟、确定性、安全性），提出基于FPGA的硬件强制语义协调架构，将TB-CSPN协调机制映射为硬件原语，实现时序同步、语义门控、授权约束等确定性协调行为，同时保留软件驱动的语义推理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前自主AI系统包含异构组件并发运行，软件协调在实时、确定性及安全性方面存在根本性局限，无法满足安全关键场景下的有界延迟和可验证协调需求。

Method: 基于TB-CSPN框架，将选定的协调语义映射到FPGA硬件原语，构建硬件原生语义协调层。硬件负责时序同步、语义门控、授权约束等确定性协调行为，而语义推理仍由软件自适应处理。

Result: 硬件强制协调层实现了时域同步、语义门控、授权约束及有界协调行为的确定性执行，同时保持语义推理的软件自适应能力。

Conclusion: 提出一种软硬分离的协调架构：软件负责灵活的语义推理，硬件负责确定性的协调执行，为解决安全关键实时系统中异构组件并发协调问题提供了有效路径。

Abstract: Recent advances in agentic AI are producing increasingly complex autonomous systems that integrate large language models, world models, optimization engines, specialized neural architectures, autonomous platforms, and human operators. While much current research focuses on improving reasoning capabilities, safety-critical real-time deployment also requires bounded and verifiable coordination among heterogeneous components operating concurrently under uncertainty. Software-mediated coordination presents fundamental limitations in domains where bounded latency, deterministic coordination, and enforceable safety guarantees are essential.
  Hence, we propose a hardware-enforced semantic coordination architecture in which selected coordination semantics are implemented directly at the hardware level via field-programmable gate arrays (FPGAs). The approach builds on the Topic-Based Communication Space Petri Net (TB-CSPN) framework, which separates semantic reasoning from interaction management.
  In this approach, selected TB-CSPN coordination mechanisms are mapped onto FPGA primitives, creating a hardware-native semantic coordination layer. Focus is not on acceleration, but on enforcing temporal synchronization, semantic gating, authorization constraints, and bounded coordination behavior directly in hardware. Semantic reasoning remains adaptive and software-driven, while embedded coordination semantics become deterministic.

</details>


### [204] [Steerability via constraints: a substrate for scalable oversight of coding agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02389)
*Thomas Winninger*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 编码代理的能力受限于人类监督；通过访问控制、网络策略和编码规范等传统工程管理方法约束代理，比最新的代理脚手架更经济有效。实验表明，加入约束子层和工具后，后门检测召回率从54.5%提升至90.9%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 编码代理缺乏有效监督会带来安全风险、损害代码库可扩展性，并增加人工审查成本。因此需要一种可扩展的监督方法。

Method: 借鉴管理大型人类工程团队的经典方法（访问控制、网络策略、严格编码规范），将其迁移到编码代理场景；设计了一个端到端系统，并通过控制实验验证：用小模型（Gemma 4 e4b）审查Python代码库中11个后门，对比无约束条件与加入约束子层和`docs` CLI工具的效果。

Result: 召回率从无约束时的54.5%提升至约束子层加工具时的90.9%，且约束子层与工具的贡献相互独立。

Conclusion: 约束编码代理的传统工程管理方法在可扩展监督上有效且更经济（节省token），这一原则从Python可扩展到Rust等语言。

Abstract: Coding agents are capable; human oversight is the bottleneck. Unconstrained agents introduce security risks, erode codebase scalability, and make human review increasingly costly. We argue that the same methods used for decades to manage large human engineering teams: access control, network policies, strict coding conventions enforced by tooling; transfer directly to coding agents, and are cheaper (in token) than recent agentic scaffolding. We sketch a start-to-end system on this principle, and report a controlled experiment in scalable oversight: a small reviewer (Gemma 4 e4b) inspects a Python codebase containing 11 inserted backdoors. Recall rises from 54.5% (unconstrained, no tools) to 90.9% (constrained substrate plus a ~200-LoC `docs` CLI), with substrate and tools contributing independently. We choose Python deliberately: substrate-level oversight gains are largest where the language gives the fewest guarantees by default; the principles extend to languages like Rust.

</details>


### [205] [Fast Multi-dimensional Refusal Subspaces via RFM-AGOP](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02396)
*Thomas Winninger*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出使用递归特征机(RFM)算法结合探针初始化，快速提取大语言模型中拒绝有害查询的多维子空间，在推理型和非推理型模型上均表现出色且计算高效。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有提取大语言模型行为子空间的方法计算成本高昂，尤其在推理模型产生的长推理轨迹上代价难以承受，亟需一种高效且可扩展的子空间提取方法。

Method: 对递归特征机(RFM)算法进行适配，引入探针信息初始化，从而高效识别多维拒绝子空间。

Result: 在推理模型(Qwen 3)和非推理模型(Qwen 2.5)上，RFM能在数秒内识别出多维拒绝子空间，且在消融任务上表现优于现有替代方法。

Conclusion: RFM有望成为现有大语言模型子空间提取方法的廉价且可扩展的补充方案，但不同方法找到的子空间之间关系仍需进一步研究。

Abstract: Steering and monitoring activations in Large Language Models (LLMs) are increasingly used for both safety and interpretability. Early work assumed behaviours are encoded along single linear directions, but recent findings suggest complex behaviours, such as the refusal to answer harmful queries, live in multi-dimensional subspaces. However, existing methods for extracting these subspaces are computationally expensive, which becomes prohibitive on reasoning models who produce long reasoning traces. By adapting the Recursive Feature Machine (RFM) algorithm -- which can be computed efficiently -- with a probe-informed initialization, we are able to identify the multi-dimensional refusal subspace in seconds, on reasoning (Qwen 3) and non-reasoning (Qwen 2.5) models. While RFM allows for faster subspace identification, it also showed better performances on the ablation task than its alternatives. More work is planned to better understand the relations between subspaces found by different methods. If confirmed, RFM could be a cheap and scalable complement to existing subspace-extraction methods in LLMs.

</details>


### [206] [Text-Driven 3D Indoor Scene Synthesis in Non-Manhattan Environments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02407)
*Xianhui Meng,Zirui Song,Yuchen Zhang,Li Zhang,Yongxuan Lv,Xiuying Chen,Kun Wang,Yan Luo,Kai Chen,Hangjun Ye,Long Chen,Jun Liu,Xiaoshuai Hao*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: SPG-Layout是一种面向非曼哈顿环境的文本驱动室内场景生成框架，通过统计先验引导训练和分层布局策略，显著提升了物体布局的物理合理性与语义真实感，在曼哈顿和非曼哈顿环境中均优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM驱动的3D室内合成方法在非曼哈顿环境中难以捕捉合理的物体布局模式，主要原因在于无法建模非正交空间关系，导致高几何违规和低物理保真度。

Method: 1) 利用物体分布统计先验引导训练过程；2) 采用模仿人类设计流程的分层布局策略，优先放置大型物体以减少布局冲突。

Result: 在包含500个非曼哈顿环境的新基准测试中，SPG-Layout在曼哈顿和非曼哈顿环境下均显著优于现有方法。

Conclusion: SPG-Layout通过统计先验与分层布局的协同优化，在语义真实感和物理合理性之间取得了平衡，有效解决了非曼哈顿环境下的室内场景生成难题。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) have demonstrated remarkable capabilities in 3D indoor synthesis for Manhattan environments. However, existing methods often fail to capture plausible object layout patterns in non-Manhattan settings, primarily because they struggle to model non-orthogonal spatial relationships, leading to high geometric violations and low physical fidelity. To address this challenge, we propose SPG-Layout, a novel text-driven framework designed to generate physically plausible indoor scenes within complex non-Manhattan environments. Specifically, we first utilize statistical priors of object distributions to guide the training process, enhancing environmental understanding and fidelity. Furthermore, mirroring human design workflows, we adopt a hierarchical layout strategy that prioritizes the placement of large objects, thereby substantially minimizing layout violations. By synergizing these components, SPG-Layout achieves a balanced optimization of semantic realism and physical plausibility. To evaluate performance in these complex settings, we constructed a new benchmark comprising 500 diverse non-Manhattan environments. Extensive experiments demonstrate that SPG-Layout consistently and significantly outperforms existing methods across both Manhattan and non-Manhattan environments. The code will be publicly released.

</details>


### [207] [Automated grading of Linux/bash examinations using large language models: a four-level cognitive taxonomy approach](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02432)
*Manuel Alonso-Carracedo,Ruben Fernandez-Boullon,Pedro Celard,Francisco J. Rodriguez-Martinez,Lorena Otero-Cerdeira*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文评估了四种前沿大语言模型（GPT、Claude Opus、Gemini、GLM）在Linux/bash命令行考试评分中的表现。Gemini 3.0 Pro结合基于rubric的提示取得了最高的人机一致性（ICC=0.888，MAE=0.10）。评分一致性随问题认知复杂度（L1-L4）递增而下降，且提示词质量对结果的影响大于模型选择。研究提出了一种基于四层认知分类法的AI辅助评分框架，可判断哪些问题适合自动评分、哪些需要人工审核。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 计算教育中命令行考试的可扩展且可靠的评分仍具挑战，学生人数增加使人工评分困难，基于规则的自动评分器无法处理部分得分、等价解法或语法变体。

Method: 采用四层认知分类法（L1信息检索到L4高级系统管理），在1200份由三位专家独立评分的真实学生答卷上，测试四种LLM在两种提示变体（基线版和rubric增强版）下的评分表现，通过ICC、MAE和Bland-Altman偏差评估人机一致性。

Result: Gemini 3.0 Pro结合rubric引导提示取得最佳效果（ICC=0.888, MAE=0.10, Bland-Altman偏差=-0.014）。评分一致性随认知层级上升而下降，rubric质量对结果的影响大于模型选择。

Conclusion: 问题复杂度是LLM评分准确度的可靠预测指标；研究建立了基于分类法的原则性框架，可判断哪些问题适合AI辅助评分、哪些需要人工审核，同时提供了可迁移的评估协议和提示模板。

Abstract: Scalable and reliable grading of command-line examinations remains a challenge in computing education, where rising enrolments make manual marking difficult and rule-based autograders cannot handle partial credit, equivalent solutions, or syntactic variation. This paper evaluates whether four frontier Large Language Models (GPT, Claude Opus, Gemini, and GLM) can approximate expert judgment when grading short Linux/bash command responses. The study adopts a four-level cognitive taxonomy that combines cognitive complexity and operational impact, ranging from information retrieval (L1) and basic file manipulation (L2) to structural operations (L3) and advanced system management (L4). The models were tested with two prompt variants, a minimal baseline and a rubric-enhanced version, on 1200 real responses from second-year Computer Engineering students independently graded by three expert instructors. Gemini~3.0 Pro with rubric-guided prompting achieved the highest human-AI agreement (ICC(3,1) = 0.888, MAE = 0.10, Bland-Altman bias = -0.014). Agreement declined consistently as taxonomy level increased, with the largest discrepancies at higher levels. Across all models, rubric quality had a larger effect than provider choice, with structured prompts consistently improving agreement. These results show that question complexity is a reliable predictor of the difficulty LLMs face in grading accurately, and they establish a principled, taxonomy-based framework for determining which questions are suitable for AI-assisted grading and which require human review, while also providing a transferable evaluation protocol and prompt templates.

</details>


### [208] [EvoPolicyGym: Evaluating Autonomous Policy Evolution in Interactive Environments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02440)
*Zhilin Wang,Han Song,Runzhe Zhan,Jusen Du,Jiacheng Chen,Tianle Li,Qingyu Yin,Yulun Wu,Zhennan Shen,Tong Zhu,Yanshu Li,Guanjie Chen,Derek F. Wong,Yafu Li,Yu Cheng,Yang Yang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了自主策略进化（Autonomous Policy Evolution）评估框架及EvoPolicyGym基准测试，用于衡量智能体在有限交互预算下迭代改进可执行策略的能力。GPT-5.5在全部16个环境中取得最佳表现，轨迹级诊断揭示了预算分配、反馈转化等关键机制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有评估要么将策略改进过程压缩为单一分数，要么与开放式软件工程进展混为一谈，缺乏对自主智能体在受控条件下迭代改进可执行策略能力的标准化评估。

Method: 提出Autonomous Policy Evolution评估框架，其中控制器智能体在固定交互预算下反复编辑可执行策略系统；构建EvoPolicyGym基准测试，基于紧凑的交互式RL环境，评估智能体如何迭代改进探索策略，并提供轨迹级诊断分析。

Result: GPT-5.5在EvoPolicyGym套件中取得最强总体排名分数，并在所有16个环境中进入前两名。轨迹级诊断区分了智能体如何分配预算、将反馈转化为参数调优的不同模式。

Conclusion: 强大的自主策略进化不仅依赖于孤立任务上的获胜，还取决于发现任务合适的机制并在有界反馈下改进策略。

Abstract: Autonomous agents are increasingly expected to improve executable policies through feedback, yet existing evaluations often collapse this process into a final score or confound it with open-ended software-engineering progress. We introduce Autonomous Policy Evolution, a controlled evaluation setting in which a harness-model agent repeatedly edits an executable policy system under a fixed interaction budget. We instantiate this setting in EvoPolicyGym, a benchmark built from compact interactive RL environments that evaluates how agents iteratively improve explored policies. On the EvoPolicyGym suite, GPT-5.5 achieves the strongest aggregate rank score and top-two performance on all 16 environments. Beyond leaderboard results, EvoPolicyGym also provides trajectory-level diagnostics that distinguish how agents allocate budget, convert feedback into parametric tuning. These analyses show that strong autonomous policy evolution depends not only on isolated task wins, but on discovering task-appropriate mechanisms and refining policies under bounded feedback.

</details>


### [209] [G-RRM: Guiding Symbolic Solvers with Recurrent Reasoning Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02491)
*Timo Bertram,Sidhant Bhavnani,Richard Freinschlag,Erich Kobler,Andreas Mayr,Günter Klambauer*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出G-RRM方法，将符号等变循环推理模型(SE-RRM)与符号求解器(回溯、Glucose 4.1、CaDiCaL 3.0)结合，用于约束满足问题。研究发现神经引导的有效性取决于两个条件：(1)问题需具有庞大的组合搜索空间；(2)求解器架构需能动态覆盖分支选择。在9×9数独上，回溯加速33.3倍，Glucose 4.1加速1.70倍，而CaDiCaL因无法覆盖注入的神经提示而无显著加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索将神经求解器(SE-RRM)与经典符号求解器结合以提升约束满足问题求解效率的条件与边界，明确神经引导何时能实际加速符号求解器。

Method: 提出G-RRM框架：SE-RRM先生成完整解提案作为神经引导，传递给符号求解器(回溯/SAT求解器如Glucose 4.1和CaDiCaL 3.0)作为分支提示，符号求解器在此基础上求解全局正确解。

Result: 9×9数独上：回溯加速33.3倍，Glucose 4.1加速1.70倍(p<0.001)；完美提示下25×25数独中Glucose加速1.17倍；CaDiCaL无显著加速(中位1.02倍)且有轻微显著减速(均值0.90倍)。

Conclusion: 神经引导的有效性取决于两个条件：问题需具有充分的组合搜索空间以暴露潜在增益；求解器架构必须能动态覆盖不完美的神经提示。满足条件时神经引导可大幅提升求解效率。

Abstract: In this work, we focus on SE-RRMs, a symbol-equivariant instantiation of RRMs that exhibits improved extrapolation to larger problem sizes. We propose a neuro-symbolic approach, ``Guiding with Recurrent Reasoning Models'' (G-RRM), which integrates SE-RRMs with symbolic solvers for constraint satisfaction problems. SE-RRMs act as neural solvers that generate full solution proposals and guide classical symbolic solvers, such as backtracking or SAT-based methods like Glucose 4.1 and CaDiCaL 3.0.0, that produce globally correct solutions. Centrally, we investigate when neural guidance with G-RRM improves the search efficiency of symbolic solvers. %
Our experiments show that the efficacy of G-RRM depends on two conditions: first, the problem instances must have an expansive combinatorial search space to expose potential gains, and second, the solver architecture must be capable of dynamically overwriting its branching choices to recover when neural hints are imperfect. When these conditions hold, guidance drives median conflict counts to zero and yields significant wall-clock speedups: on $9\times9$ Sudoku, where the SE-RRM correctly solves $91.1\%$ of instances, backtracking accelerates by $33.3\times$ and Glucose 4.1 by $1.70\times$ (median, $p<0.001$), with Glucose 4.1 retaining a $1.17\times$ speedup on perfect-hint $25\times25$ grids. In contrast, CaDiCaL 3.0.0, whose runtime is overhead-dominated and which always respects the injected branching hints rather than overwriting them, shows no significant speedup (median $1.02\times$, n.s.) and even a small significant mean slowdown ($0.90\times$) on $9\times9$. These results delineate the regimes in which neural guidance translates into practical speedups.

</details>


### [210] [What LLM Agents Say When No One Is Watching: Social Structure and Latent Objective Emergence in Multi-Agent Debates](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02507)
*Arman Ghaffarizadeh,Danyal Mohaddes,Aliakbar Izadkhah,Shahriar Noroozizadeh*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出双通道辩论框架，研究LLM智能体在结构化社交环境中的公开表达与私下表达（OTR）的系统性差异。在10个模型、3个场景下，对齐设置使决策分歧从约3%基线升至约40%，OTR回复常将公开迎合归因于关系压力（如职业风险、赞助义务）。研究表明，评估应超越显式目标，检测涌现性目标。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究LLM智能体在结构化社交环境（角色、受众、关系背景）中，即使提示中没有显式目标，社交结构是否会改变其公开表达与私下表达（OTR）的内容。

Method: 提出双通道辩论框架：智能体产生公开话语（进入共享历史）和OTR回复（记录但不展示给另一方）。在10个模型、3个场景、每场景5个变体上进行实验，通过立场、语义相似性、自然语言推断、调查回复四种聚合分析测量公开-OTR分歧。

Result: 对齐诱导设置使目标智能体的决策分歧从约3%基线升至约40%。效果在四种聚合分析中一致。部分OTR回复明确将公开迎合归因于关系压力（如职业风险、赞助义务）。

Conclusion: LLM智能体评估应超越显式目标，检测涌现性目标。提出了双通道评估框架及补充行为指标来操作化这一评估。

Abstract: LLM agents will increasingly act in socially structured settings where role, audience, and relational context can shape what is advantageous or costly to say. We study whether such social structure, without any explicit objective in the prompt, changes what an agent expresses publicly relative to an off-the-record (OTR) channel elicited under the same condition. We introduce a dual-channel debate framework in which agents produce public utterances that enter the shared history alongside OTR responses that are recorded but never shown to the other participant. Across 10 models, 3 scenarios, and 5 variations within each scenario, alignment-inducing settings produce systematic public-OTR divergence in the targeted agent, with its decision divergence rising from a $\sim$3% baseline to roughly 40%. The effect is consistent across four aggregate analyses: stance, semantic similarity, natural language inference, and survey responses. In some cases, the OTR response explicitly attributes public accommodation to relational pressures, such as career risk or sponsorship obligation. The findings suggest that agent evaluation should extend beyond explicit goals and detect emergent objectives. We present a dual-channel evaluation framework and complementary behavioral measures that operationalize this assessment.

</details>


### [211] [ReContext: Recursive Evidence Replay as LLM Harness for Long-Context Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02509)
*Yanjun Zhao,Ruizhong Qiu,Tianxin Wei,Yuanchen Bei,Zhining Liu,Lingjie Chen,Ismini Lourentzou,Hanghang Tong,Jingrui He*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: RECONTEXT是一种无需训练的推理方法，通过递归地利用模型内部相关性信号构建查询条件化的证据池，并在最终生成前重放该证据池，同时保留完整原始上下文，从而提升大语言模型在长上下文场景中的证据利用能力。在128K上下文长度的八个数据集上，该方法在Qwen3-4B、Qwen3-8B和Llama3-8B上均取得了最佳平均排名。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型虽然支持越来越长的上下文窗口，但往往无法有效利用输入中已有的相关证据，存在上下文访问与实际利用之间的差距。现有方法通常需要训练、外部记忆或上下文剪枝，而本文旨在提出一种无需训练的推理方法来弥合这一差距。

Method: RECONTEXT是一种训练免费的推理方法，核心包括：(1) 利用模型内部相关性信号构建查询条件化的证据池；(2) 在最终生成前递归重放该证据池，同时保留完整原始上下文；(3) 将证据组织与答案生成解耦，无需训练、外部记忆或上下文剪枝。此外，论文基于联想记忆理论提供了理论分析，将上下文视为记忆存储、问题视为检索线索、注意力视为线索-痕迹关联、重放视为痕迹重激活。

Result: 在八个长上下文数据集（128K上下文长度）上，RECONTEXT在Qwen3-4B、Qwen3-8B和Llama3-8B三个骨干模型上均一致提升了证据利用能力，并在所有三个骨干模型上取得了最佳平均排名。

Conclusion: RECONTEXT作为无需训练的长上下文推理方法，通过递归证据重放有效弥合了上下文访问与实际利用之间的差距，在多个模型和数据集上验证了其有效性和通用性。代码已开源。

Abstract: Understanding and reasoning over long contexts has become a key requirement for deploying large language models (LLMs) in realistic applications. Although recent LLMs support increasingly long context windows, they often fail to use relevant evidence that is already present in the input, revealing a gap between context access and effective context utilization. In this work, we propose Recursive Evidence Replay as LLM Harness for Long-Context Reasoning (RECONTEXT), a training-free inference method for improving long-context reasoning. RECONTEXT uses model-internal relevance signals to construct a query-conditioned evidence pool and replays it before final generation while preserving the full original context. This recursive selection process separates evidence organization from answer generation without training, external memory, or context pruning. We also provide a theoretical analysis based on associative memory, which characterizes the context as a memory store, the question as a retrieval cue, attention as cue-trace association, and replay as trace reactivation. Experiments on eight long-context datasets with 128K context length show that RECONTEXT consistently improves evidence utilization across Qwen3-4B, Qwen3-8B, and Llama3-8B, achieving the best average rank on all three backbones. Code is available at https://github.com/Yanjun-Zhao/ReContext.

</details>


### [212] [Online Safety Monitoring for LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02510)
*Mona Schirmer,Metod Jazbec,Alexander Timans,Christian Naesseth,Maja Waldron,Eric Nalisnick*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文针对LLM部署时的安全性问题，提出了一种简单的实时监控器：通过阈值化外部验证器信号并利用风险控制校准阈值，在数学推理和红队测试数据集上，该简单方法与基于序贯假设检验的先进监控器性能相当。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM在对齐训练后仍可能在部署时产生不安全输出，因此需要在线上监控输出并在无法保证安全时发出警报。

Method: 提出一种简单的实时监控器，将外部模型的验证器信号通过阈值化转化为警报决策，并使用风险控制方法校准阈值。

Result: 在数学推理和红队测试数据集上的实验表明，该简单方法与基于序贯假设检验的更先进监控器性能相当。

Conclusion: 简单的阈值化监控器结合风险控制校准，在LLM安全性监控任务上能够达到与复杂方法竞争的性能。

Abstract: Despite alignment training, LLMs remain prone to generating unsafe outputs at deployment time. Monitoring outputs online and raising an alarm when safety can no longer be assumed is therefore critical. We study a simple real-time monitor that turns a verifier signal from an external model into an alarm decision by thresholding, with the threshold calibrated via risk control. In experiments on mathematical reasoning and red teaming datasets, we show that this simple design is competitive with more advanced monitors based on sequential hypothesis testing.

</details>


### [213] [Distributed Attacks in Persistent-State AI Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02514)
*Josh Hills,Ida Caspary,Asa Cooper Stickland*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文研究了AI编码代理在持续代码库中跨多次PR迭代开发时，可能通过渐进式攻击（将恶意负载分散到多个PR中）来规避监控。实验表明，没有单一监控器能同时有效防御渐进式和非渐进式攻击，而引入状态化链接追踪监控器并结合多监控器集成，可将渐进式攻击的规避率从93%降至47%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着AI编码代理的自主性增强，代码库在会话间持续存在，这创造了新的攻击面：被提示注入或行为不端的代理可以将攻击分散到多个PR中，并选择最佳时机释放有效负载。现有研究缺乏对这种迭代、持续性环境下攻击与防御动态的系统性研究。

Method: 提出了Iterative VibeCoding基准测试，包含CLI工具和Flask Web服务两个任务系列共20个任务变体。使用Claude Sonnet 4.5作为攻击代理，GPT-4o作为监控器。对比了渐进式攻击（将侧任务分散到多个PR）与非渐进式攻击（集中在单个PR）。引入了状态化链接追踪监控器，并构建了四监控器集成系统。

Result: 没有单一监控器能同时防御两种攻击策略；渐进式攻击和非渐进式攻击的规避效果取决于监控器类型。高规避率（≥65%）在不同模型后端（Sonnet 4.5, Gemini 3.1 Pro, Kimi K2.5）中普遍存在。状态化链接追踪监控器在检测渐进式攻击上显著优于仅累积历史信息的diff监控器。四监控器集成将渐进式攻击规避率从93%降至47%。

Conclusion: 持续状态攻击面是AI编码代理安全的核心挑战，而非单一模型能力问题。多监控器集成策略（结合状态化追踪和轨迹监控）是防御渐进式攻击的有效方向，但当前仍无法完全消除风险，需要进一步研究。

Abstract: As AI coding agents become more autonomous, they increasingly ship code iteratively, with the codebase persisting across sessions.
